tolkāppiya urittoki
tolkāppiyam nam vāḻviyal āvaṇam; nam muntiyar kaṇṭa moḻiyiyal vaḷaṅkaḷ aṉittiyum tiraṭṭit tanta tēṉ tēṭṭu!
attēṭṭic cuvitta kōttumpikaḷ paḻiya uriyāciriyarkaḷ. avarkaḷ urikaḷiyellām oṉṟu tiraṭṭi, variciyuṟat tamiḻulakam koḷḷa vitta urittokutikaḷ iviyām!
muṉṉip patippāciriyarkaḷ teḷivuṟuttum maṇikkuvikaḷ ellāmum, avarkaḷ vitta vippuppaṭi vitta ceppēṭukaḷ it tolkāppiya urit tokiyām. mēlum, ivi kiṭittaṟku ariya karuvūlamumām!
***** ***** *****
iḷampūraṇar, cēṉāvariyar, pērāciriyar, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar,  teyvac ciliyār, kallāṭar ākiya paḻiya uriyāciriyarkaḷ, uri viḷakkam pala kālaṅkaḷil eḻutiyuḷar. eḻuttu, col, poruḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ patippukaḷ orē kālattil oruvarāl veḷiyiṭappaṭa villi. avaṟṟiyellām ōriṭattil orē vēḷiyil oṭṭu mottamāka muṉṉip patippāciriyarkaḷ uri - viḷakkam - kuṟippu - iṇippu ākiyavaṟṟuṭaṉ peṟa veḷiyiṭum ariya periya muyaṟciyil veḷiyiṭappaṭuvatu tamiḻmaṇ patippakattiṉ ippatippākum. tolkāppiyattiṟkuk kiṭitta urikaḷ aṉittiyum orumottamākat taruvatāl itu, tolkāppiya urittoki eṉappaṭṭatu. tokiyāvatu tokukkappaṭṭatu. 
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ



aṟimukavuri
va.u. citamparaṉār
vaḷḷi nāyaka - ulakanāta - citamparaṉār,  `va.u.ci.’ eṉa mūṉṟeḻuttil muttamiḻp pukaḻum koṇṭavar avar!
“nāṭum moḻiyum  nammiru kaṇkaḷ
eṉṟu koṇṭa perumakkaḷi viral viṭṭu maṭakkaka eṇṇiṉ - avaṟṟukkē tammi īkam ceytavari eṇṇiṉ - aṭutta viralila maṭakka - āḻamāka eṇṇit tāṉē āka vēṇṭum!
ōṭṭappiṭārattil, ulakanātar - paramāyiyar makaṉārāka 05.09.1892 il piṟantavar va.u.ci. tantiyār vaḻakkaṟiñar:
tāmpiṟanta ūri irunta `vīrap perumāḷ aṇṇāvi’ eṉpāriṭam tamiḻ ilakkiya ilakkaṇaṅkaraḷik kaṟṟār. tūttukkuṭi kiṟittava uyarpaḷḷi, tirunelvēli maturi tiraviyam tāyumāṉavar intuk kallūri uyarpaḷḷi āyavaṟṟil kaṟṟār.
tantiyār kālṭuvel kallūriyil cērttup payilac ceytum, kalvi nāṭṭam illārāy ūrkku vantu vaṭṭa āṭciyar aluvalakattil eḻuttar paṇi ceytār. atuvum ēṟkāmal caṭṭam paṭikkat tiruccirāppaḷḷi vantu caṭṭak kallūriyil payiṉṟu ki.pi. 1895il vaḻakkaṟiñarāṉār. paṭikkum pōtē 1894il tirumaṇamum āyatu. maṇamakaṉār vaḷḷiyammi. vaṟiyavarkku vātāṭalum, kāvalarit tiṇaṟavikka viṉāvalum koṇṭa va.u.ci.yiṉmēl, araciṉpaki maḷiyiṭat toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu. tantiyār tūttukkuṭikkuc cella vittār.
vaḷḷiyammi 1900il iyaṟki eyta, avar uṟaviṉarāya mīṉāṭciyammiyi maṇakkavum nērntatu.
citamparaṉār tūttukkuṭikkuc ceṉṟāl eṉṉa? avar īkamum tuṇivum avaruṭaṉ tāṉē irukkum! tūttukkuṭi perunakar! toṇṭukkum vāyppu! ekiptu koli vaḻakku! viṭuviṭuppu muṭipu! ēḻimiyar tōḻami yarāṉār!
tamiḻil toyvum, civa camaya īṭupāṭum perukap peruvāyppu ēṟpaṭa `vivēkapāṉu’ eṉṉum itaḻ naṭattiṉār. itu mātiki itaḻ.
ceṉṉikkuc ceṉṟu tirumpum oru cūḻal ēṟpaṭṭatu. appoḻutu citamparkku ōr eḻucci uṇṭāyatu; vaṇikattāl nērnta ayalārāṭciyi oḻikka, avvaṇikamē vaḻi eṉṟu tiṭṭam tōṉṟik kappal iyakkutalil muṉintātar.
mumpi ceṉṟu kappal vāṅki, tūttukkuṭikkum keḻumpukkum iṭiyē celuttiṉār. `cutēcik kappal’ āṅkilarik kotikka vikkātā? cutēci paṇṭacāli, neytal cāli, eṉpavaṟṟi niṟuviṉār. ālip pōrāṭṭaṅkaḷil muṉṉiṉṟār. tilakarit talivarākak koṇṭār. cūrattil nikaḻnta mānāṭṭil paṅkukoṇṭu (1907) mīṇṭu mēṭikaḷil araciyal muḻakkamiṭṭār. cuppiramaṇiyē civā uṭaṉāṉār! māvaṭṭa āṭciyar viñcu eṉpār citamparaṉār, civā iruvar mītum kuṟṟaṅkaḷ pala cāṟṟi, nāṭukaṭattavum tiṭṭamiṭṭār.
muṟimaṉṟam 20 āṇṭu, kaṭuṅkāval taṇṭam vitikka, ataṉi `iṟivaṉ aruḷ’ eṉṟā. mēl muṟiyīṭukaḷāl, āṟāṇṭukaḷ ākik kaṇalpoṟi iyakki, cekkiḻuttu 1912il viṭutali peṟṟār.
ciṟiyil cēmcu ālaṉ nūlkaḷi moḻi peyarttār. viṭutalikkuppiṉ tamiḻt toṇṭil āḻamāka iṟaṅkiṉār. tolkāppiya iḷampūraṇar uriyi 1936il patippittār. tirukkuṟaḷ aṟattuppāl virivuri 1935il veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. avartam āyvum tuṇivum tirukkuṟaḷ uriyil veḷippaṭṭatu. avar eṇṇiyavāṟu muḻuturi kaṇṭum muḻutuṟa veḷippaṭavilli!
ciṟiyil avar eḻutiya `cuyacariti’yiṉ arumi paṭippār neñci urukkum! accariti 1946il pāri niliyattiṉarāl veḷiyiṭappaṭṭup pala patippukaḷ kaṇṭatu.
tirukkuṟaḷ aṟattuppāl uri, ciṅkappūr tamiḻttiru. kōvalaṅ kaṇṇaṉārāl veḷiyiṭappaṭṭu kuṟaḷ kūṟiya ovvoruvarukkum ilavayamāka vaḻaṅkiyatu. va.u.ci. yiṉ vaṇmik katireihḷi eṉat tōṉṟiyatu. 14ām māṭiyil veḷiyiṭṭu avvuri nayam eḷiyēṉ kūṟa, avvurip perumi aviyit tiḷikkac ceytatu!
kapparēlāṭṭiya tamiḻar - tamiḻk kappalum ōṭṭiya
tōṉṟal - tam nilikuṟittu oruveṇpāvil tāmē
ōṭukiṟār; ōṭṭappiṭārattār allarō!
“vantakavi ñarkkellām māriyeṉap palporuḷum
tanta citamparaṉ tāḻntiṉṟu- cantamiḻ veṇ
pāc collip piccikkup pārellām ōṭukiṉṟāṉ
nāc collum tōlum nalintu”
“ē! tāḻnta tamiḻakamē! eṉṟatu meytāṉē!
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ.

tolkāppiyam ariya patippukaḷum tēvikaḷum
pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṭip pakutiyil  tamiḻiṉ paṇṭi ilakkaṇa/ ilakkiya nūlkaḷ acciṭap peṟṟaṉa. avvakiyil tamiḻil accaṭikkappaṭṭa mutal ilakkaṇa nūlāka iṉṟu aṟiyappaṭuvatu naṉṉūlē.    kāraṇam tamiḻaka maṭaṅkaḷ naṉṉūlip payiṟṟilakkaṇa mākavum payaṉ pāṭṭu ilakkaṇamākavum koṇṭiruntaṉa.  1835 -il tiruttaṇiki vicākap perumāḷiyar tāmē uriyeḻuti naṉṉūl mūlamum kāṇṭikiyuriyum eṉṉum talippil veḷiyiṭṭār. toṭarntu tāṇṭavarāya mutaliyārum,a. muttuccāmi piḷḷiyum iṇintu ilakkaṇap pañcakam eṉṉum talippil naṉṉūl,  nampiyakap poruḷ, puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāli ākiyavaṟṟiṉ mūlaṅkaḷi veḷik koṇarn taṉar. itaṉ toṭarcciyāka 1838 -il vīramā muṉivariṉ toṉṉūl viḷakkam accākkam peṟṟatu. 1847 -il tāṉ maḻavi makāliṅkiyarāl tamiḻiṉ tol ilakkaṇam tolkāppiya eḻuttatikāram uriyōṭu acciṭappaṭṭatu. 
toṭarntu yāḻppāṇattu nallūr āṟumukanāvalar, yāḻppāṇattu ci.vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷi, kōmaḷapuram irācakōpālapiḷḷi, cōṭacāvatāṉam cupparāya ceṭṭiyār, pōṉṟōr 19 -ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, tolkāppiyattik kiṭitta urikaḷōṭu patippittaṉar. 1935 - il tolkāppiyat tiṟku eḻutappaṭṭa urikaḷ  (iṉṟu kiṭittuḷḷa aṉittu urikaḷum) patippikkappaṭṭu viṭṭaṉa. 1847 - 2003 vari tolkāppiyam eḻuttatikāram nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uri eṭṭup patippukaḷum, 1885-2003 vari tolkāppiyam poruḷatikāram nacciṉārkkiṉiyar muṉṉintiyal eṭṭup patippukaḷum, ceyyuḷiyal nacciṉārkkiṉiyar taṉittu mūṉṟu patippukaḷum veḷivantuḷḷaṉa. 1892 - 2003 vari tolkāppiyam collatikāram nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uri aintu patippukaḷum, 1868 - 2006 vari eḻuttu iḷampūraṇam ēḻu patippukaḷum, 1920 - 2005 vari tolkāppiyam poruḷatikāram iḷampūraṇam pattup patippukaḷum, 1927 - 2005 vari tolkāppiyam collatikāram iḷampūraṇam āṟu patippukaḷum, 1885 - 2003 vari tolkāppiyam poruḷatikāram pērāciriyam ēḻu patippukaḷum, 1929 - 2003 vari tolkāppiyam collatikāram teyvacciliyār mūṉṟu patippukaḷum, 1964 - 2003 vari tolkāppiyam collatikāram kallāṭam mūṉṟu patippukaḷum veḷivantuḷḷaṉa eṉa muṉivar pā. matukēvaraṉ tamatu tolkāppiyam patippu āvaṇam nūlil  kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
2003 -il tamiḻmaṇ patippakattiṉ vāyilākat tolkāppiya urikaḷ (iḷampūraṇam, nacciṉārkkiṉiyam, pērāciriyam, cēṉāvariyam, kallāṭam, teyvacciliyam) paṇṭita vitvāṉ ti.vē. kōpāliyar, muṉivar na. araṇamuṟuval ākiyōrip patippāciriyarākak koṇṭu, mutumuṉivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉāriṉ vāḻviyal viḷakkattuṭaṉ veḷivantuḷḷaṉa. 
iruppiṉum accil iṉṟu kiṭikkāta  ariya patippukaḷi mīḷ patippākap patippikka vēṇṭum eṉṉum nōkkattil, ci.vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷi patippu, kā. namaccivāya mutaliyār patippu, va.u. citamparam piḷḷi patippu, irā. veṅkaṭācalam piḷḷi patippu, ci. kaṇēciyar patippu,  te.po. mīṉāṭci cuntaraṉār patippu  ākiyōriṉ patippukaḷuṭaṉ tolkāppiyam toṭarpāka aṟiñar perumakkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷa āyvuk kaṭṭurikaḷum iṇittu tamiḻmaṇ patippakattiṉ vaḻi mīḷ patippukaḷākap patippikkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
mīḷ patippil piṉpaṟṟiyuḷḷa potu neṟikaḷ
 mutumuṉivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉāriṉ tolkāppiyam kuṟitta viḷakkam, ovvōr atikāram paṟṟiya vāḻviyal viḷakkam, uriyāciriyar vivaram ākiyavi nūliṉ toṭakkat til tokuttu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. muṉṉip patippukaḷiṉ mukappup pakkaṅkaḷ koṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. muṉṉip patippāciriyar paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ kāla aṭippaṭiyil tokuttaḷikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ovvōr iyaliṉ  toṭakkattil,   pērāciriyar ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉār tolkāppiyam tokuppil eḻuti yuḷḷa tolkāppiya iyal paṟṟiya viḷakkam tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. muṉṉip patippil  aṭikkuṟippukaḷ anta antap pakkattil tarappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ippatippil ovvōr iyalukkum toṭar  ilakkamiṭṭu, antanta iyaliṉ iṟutiyil tokuttu  vaḻaṅkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
	muṉṉip patippukaḷil nūṟpākkaḷik kiyāṇṭuḷḷa muṟiyiyē ip patippilum piṉpaṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. nūṟpāk kaḷukkut tamiḻ eṇ koṭukkappaṭṭiruppiṉ avi  naṭi muṟiyil ippōtu uḷḷa eṇkaḷāka māṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. nūṟpā akaramutali, mēṟkōḷ akaramutali pōṉṟavaṟṟiṟkup pakka eṇkaḷ koṭukkappaṭṭiruppiṉ avi nūṟpā eṇkaḷāka māṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
piṉṉiṇippākat tolkāppiyam toṭarpāka aṟiñar perumakkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷa  kaṭṭurikaḷ iṇikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. nūṟpā mutaṟ kuṟippu akarāti maṭṭum koṭukkap paṭṭuḷḷa patippukaḷil avaṟṟōṭu, 2003 tamiḻmaṇ patippakattiṉ vaḻiyē paṇṭita vittuvāṉ ti.vē. kōpāliyar muṉivar na. araṇamuṟuval ākiyōrip patippāciriyarkaḷākak koṇṭu veḷivanta ilakkaṇap patippil iṇikkap paṭṭirunta, 
-	nūṟpā niral
-	mēṟkōḷ col niral
-	mēṟkōḷ coṟṟoṭar niral
-	ceyyuḷ mēṟkōḷ niral
-	kalic col niral( nūṟpāvaḻi)
-	kaliccol  niral (urivaḻi)
ākiyavi eṭuttup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
-	pāṭavēṟupāṭukaḷ cuṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
-	muṉṉip patippil vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷ payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭiruppiṉ avi appaṭiyē kiyāḷap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
-	tolkāppiyap patippukaḷ kuṟitta vivaram tokukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ci.vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷi patippu  - 1892
collatikāram -  nacciṉārkkiṉiyar 	
1868 - il tolkāppiyam collatikāram cēṉāvari yaruriyip patippitta ci.vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷi, 1892 ām āṇṭu collatikāram nacciṉārkiṉiyar uriyip patippittār. collatikāram nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uri toṭarcciyākap patippikkappaṭṭālum, mutalil patippitta ci.vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷiyiṉ patippu iṉṟu accuvaṭivil puḻakkattil illi. appatippiṉi veḷiyiṭum nōkkattil ippatippu mīḷ patippāka veḷivarukiṟatu. ippatippil nūṟpā mutaṟkuṟippu akarāti maṭṭum koṭukkappaṭṭiruntatu. attuṭaṉ maṟṟa iṇippukaḷum iṇikkappaṭṭu, mutumuṉivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ avarkaḷiṉ vāḻviyal viḷakkamum, ovvōr iyaliṉ toṭakkattil ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉār eḻutiyuḷḷa iyal viḷakkamum koṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. attuṭaṉ collatikāram toṭarpāka aṟiñar perumakkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷa āyvuk kaṭṭurikaḷ piṉṉiṇippāka iṇikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. 
kā. namaccivāya mutaliyār patippu - 1927
collatikāram - iḷampūraṇam
	 iḷampūraṇam eḻuttatikāram poruḷatikāram urikaḷ muṉpē veḷivantaṉa. collatikāram   iḷampūraṇar uri 1927 - il kāvērippākkam namaccivāya mutaliyāriṉ ārāyccik kuṟippukaḷuṭaṉ ceṉṉi si. kumāracāmi nāyuṭu saṉsāl  veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. ituvē collatikāram iḷampūraṇar uriyiṉ mutaṟ patippākum. ip patippil aṭikkuṟippukaḷ antantap pakkattil koṭukkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ippatippil toṭar  ilakkamiṭṭu, ovvōr iyaliṉ muṭivil  aṭik kuṟippukaḷ tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ip patippil   nūṟpākkaḷukkut tamiḻ eṇvariciyum naṭimuṟi eṇ variciyum koṭukkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ippatippil naṭimuṟi eṇ muṟi maṭṭum piṉpaṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. 
va.u. citamparampiḷḷi patippu - 1928 , 1931, 1933, 1935
eḻuttatikāram, poruḷatikāram - iḷampūraṇam
1868 il tiricirapuram cōṭacāvatāṉam cupparāya ceṭṭiyārip patippāciriyarākak koṇṭu kaṉṉiyappa mutaliyārāl patippikkappaṭṭatu.  itaṉi aṭuttu 1928 -il tolkāppiyam iḷampūraṇam eḻuttatikāram patavuri eṉṉum peyaril va.u. citamparam piḷḷi patippu vēlāyutam piriṇṭiṅ pira, tūttukkuṭiyiliruntu veḷivantatu. appatippiṉ toṭakkattil koṭukkappaṭṭirunta  nūṟpā akarāti ippatippiṉ nūliṉ iṟutiyil koṭukkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. attuṭaṉ col niral (mēṟkōḷ), coṟṟoṭar niral (mēṟkōḷ), ceyyuḷ niral (mēṟkōḷ) kalic col niral (nūṟpā vaḻi) kalic col niral (urivaḻi) tokuttuk koṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
1920 -il kā. namaccivāya mutaliyār poruḷatikāram iḷampūraṇam uri mutal pakutiyākak kaḷaviyal, kaṟpiyal, poruḷiyal mūṉṟu iyalkaḷiyum si. kumāracāmi nāyuṭu saṉ niṟuvaṉattār veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār. maṟṟa pakutikaḷ veḷivantatāka aṟiya iyalavilli. ippatippiṟkup piṉ va.u. citamparam piḷḷi tolkāppiyam iḷampūraṇam poruḷatikāram akattiṇiyiyal, puṟattiṇiyiyal ākiya iru iyalkaḷ iṇinta patippu, ceṉṉip pirampūril iruntu veḷivantatu. ippatippil āṇṭu kuṟippiṭappaṭavilli. āṉāl 1921- il veḷivanta kuṟippiṉi, tolkāppiyam iḷampūraṇam eḻuttikāram patavuri patippil kuṟippiṭukiṟār. kaḷaviyal, kaṟpiyal, poruḷiyal 1933 - il vāviḷḷa irāmavāmi cāṭrulu aṇṭ saṉ mūlam veḷi vantuḷḷatu. 1935 - il mēṟkaṇṭa patippāḷarkaḷik koṇṭu meyppāṭṭiyal, uvamiyiyal, ceyyuḷiyal, marapiyal patippittuḷḷār. mūṉṟu pakutikaḷāka veḷivantālum  avaṟṟiṟkum toṭar eṇ koṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  1935 - il oruṅkiṇinta patippāka va.u. citamparam piḷḷi e. viyāpurippiḷḷi ākiyōr patippāciriyarkaḷāka amintu veḷivantuḷḷatu.  taṉittaṉip patippukaḷāka mūṉṟu pakutikaḷāka  veḷivantuḷḷa patippiṉi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu im mīḷ patippu patippikkappaṭukiṉṟatu.
irā. veṅkaṭācalam piḷḷi patippu  - 1929
collatikāram  - teyvac ciliyār
 1929 -il karantit tamiḻc caṅkattāl mutaṉ mutalil patippikkappaṭṭatu. patippāciriyar rā. vēṅkaṭācalam piḷḷi. itaṉ niḻaṟpaṭivam 1984 -il tamiḻp palkalikkaḻakam veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu.  ip patippil cūttira mutaṟ kuṟippu akarāti maṭṭum koṭukkappaṭṭiruntatu. attuṭaṉ col niral (mēṟkōḷ), coṟṟoṭar niral (mēṟkōḷ), ceyyuḷ niral (mēṟkōḷ) kalic col niral (nūṟpā vaḻi) kalic col niral ( urivaḻi) tokuttuk koṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ci. kaṇēciyar patippu - 1937, 1938, 1943,1948
eḻuttu nacciṉārkkiṉārkiṉiyar, collatikāram  cēṉāvariyar poruḷ -  nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, pērāciriyar
1847 - il maḻavi makāliṅkiyarāl eḻuttatikāram nacciṉārk kiṉiyam patippikkappaṭṭatu. 1891 - il ci. vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷi patippum 1923 - il ta. kaṉakacuntarattiṉ patippum kaḻakattiṉ mūlamum veḷivantaṉa. 1937 - il nā. poṉṉiyā avarkaḷāl kaṇēciyar eḻutiya uriviḷakkak kuṟippukaḷuṭaṉ eḻuttatikāram mūlamum nacciṉārkkiṉiyaruriyum veḷiyiṭappaṭṭaṉa.
1868  -il yāḻppāṇam nallūr āṟumuka nāvalar patippu, ceṉṉapaṭṭaṇam  ū. puṣparatac ceṭṭiyāratu kalāratnākara accukkūṭattil ci.vi. tāmōratam piḷḷiyāl patippikkappaṭṭatu. itē āṇṭu kōmaḷapuram irācakōpāla piḷḷiyiṉ patippum tolkāppiyam cēṉāvariyam eṉṟa peyaril veḷivantuḷḷatu. 1923 - il kantacāmiyār tiruttiya tiruttaṅkaḷōṭum kuṟippuriyōṭum kaḻakap patippāka veḷivantatu. 1938 -il ci. kaṇēciyar patippu avar eḻutiya uriviḷakkak kuṟippukaḷuṭaṉ veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu.
1885 -il ci.vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷiyāl tolkāppiyam poruḷatikāram nacciṉārkkiṉiyar iyaṟṟiya uriyōṭum palatēcap piratirūpaṅkaḷik koṇṭu paricōtittu yāḻppāṇam ci.vi. tāmōtararāl patippikkappaṭṭatu eṉṉum kuṟippuṭaṉ patippu veḷivantatu. appatippil uḷḷa piṉṉāṉkiyal nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uri aṉṟu eṉṟu maṟuttu irā. irākaviyaṅkār 1902 - 1905 variyāṉa centamiḻ itaḻil āyvukkaṭṭuri eḻutit teḷivupaṭuttiṉār.  ataṉit toṭarntu poruḷatikāram muḻutum nāṉku pakutiyāka nacciṉārkkiṉiyam, pērāciriyam eṉap pirittu 1916, 1917 - ām āṇṭukaḷil ca. pavaṉāntam piḷḷi patippittār. 1917 - il rā. irākaviyaṅkār ceyyuḷiyalukku nacciṉārkkiyar uri iruppati aṟintu, nacciṉārkiṉiyar uri uriyāciriyar uriyuṭaṉ eṉa iru urikaḷiyum iṇittu veḷiyiṭṭār. 1934, 1935 -il e. viyāpurippiḷḷi avarkaḷāl ōlip pirati paricōtikkappaṭṭu mā.nā. cōmacuntaram piḷḷi avarkaḷatu ariya ārāyccik kuṟippukaḷuṭaṉum tiruttaṅ kaḷuṭaṉum tolkāppiyam poruḷatikāram mutaṟpākam veḷivantatu. 1948 - il tolkāppiya muṉivarāl iyaṟṟap paṭṭa tolkāppiyam poruḷatikāram (mutaṟpākam) muṉṉintiyalum nacciṉārkkiṉiyamum eṉa, ci. kaṇēciyar eḻutiya ārāyccik kuṟippuriyuṭaṉ nā. poṉṉiyā avarkaḷāl patippikkappaṭṭatu. 1943 -il tolkāppiyam poruḷatikāram (iraṇṭām pākam) eṉpatōṭu piṉṉāṉkiyalum pērāciriyamum ivi puṉṉālik kaṭṭuvaṉ tamiḻ vittuvāṉ, piramarī ci. kaṇēciyar avarkaḷ ēṭṭup piratikaḷōṭu oppunōkkit tiruttiya tiruttaṅkaḷōṭum eḻutiya uri viḷakkak kuṟippukaḷōṭum īḻakēcari atipar nā. poṉṉiyā avarkaḷāl tamatu cuṉṉākam, tirumakaḷ aḻuttakattil patippikkapaṭṭaṉa eṉa variyaṟukkap paṭṭiruntatu.
2007-il ulakat tamiḻārāycci niṟuvaṉam maṟupatippāka ci. kaṇēciyariṉ patippip patittuḷḷatu. appatippiṉip piṉpaṟṟiyē ippatippu uruvākkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  cūttira akarāti, cūttira arumpata viḷakkam ākiyavaṟṟiṟku ippatippil pakka eṇ koṭukkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. avi im mīḷpatippil nūṟpā eṇkaḷāka māṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. aṉupantamāka ci. kaṇēciyyar cila kaṭṭurikaḷi appatippil iṇit tiruntār avaṟṟōṭu mēlum avaruṭiya cila kaṭṭurikaḷ ippatippil iṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. 
te.po. mīṉāṭci cuntaraṉār patippu  1971
collatikāram kallāṭam 
ci.vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷi tolkāppiyam poruḷati kāram nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uri patippuriyil (1885) kallāṭar uri paṟṟiya kuṟippiṉit tarukiṟār.  ṭi.eṉ appaṉiyaṅkār centamiḻ itaḻil (1920, tokuti-19, pakuti-1, pakkam-20) kallāṭaruri eṉṉum kaṭṭuriyil rīmāṉ em cēkṣakiri cātiriyār (1893) kallāṭar uri kuṟittu ārāyntuḷḷatākak kuṟippiṭukiṟār. cōḻavantāṉ aracañcaṇmukaṉār (1905) rā. irākaviyaṅkār (1917) peyar viḻiyāṉ, kā. namaccivāya mutaliyār(1920) navanītakiruṣṇa pārati(1920) pi. citampara puṉṉivaṉanāta mutaliyār(1922) kantacāmiyār (1923) va.u. cimparam piḷḷi (1928) maṉṉārkuṭi tamiḻp paṇṭitar ma.nā. cōmacuntaram piḷḷi (1929) araṅka. vēṅkaṭācalam piḷḷi (1929) ākiyōr kallāṭar uri kuṟitta kuṟippukaḷit tamatu kaṭṭurikaḷil kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉar.  piṉṉaṅkuṭi cā. cuppiramaṇiya cātiriyār kallāṭar uri ōriyaṇṭal kiyeḻuttup puttaka cāliyil uḷḷatu eṉṟum, avvuri evviyal vari uḷḷatu eṉṉum kuṟippuriyum tarukiṟār.
1950 - 1952  vari tarumapura ātīṉam veḷiyiṭṭa ñāṉa campantam itaḻil araciṉar kīḻtticic cuvaṭi nūlakattiṉ iraṇṭām piratiyi oṭṭip patippikkappaṭṭatu. kiḷaviyākkam toṭaṅki iṭiyiyal 10 - āvatu nūṟpā vari veḷivantatu. kallāṭar uriyit tammuṭiya tiruttaṅkaḷuṭaṉ veḷiyiṭṭavar makāvittuvāṉ taṇṭapāṇi tēcikar. 1963 -il tolkāppiyam collatikāram urikkōvi eṉṉum peyaril āpirakām aruḷappaṉum va.ai. cuppiramaṇiyamum iṇintu veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. itu nāṉku iyalukkāṉa patippāka amintiruntatu. kaḻakap patippāka 1964 -il ku. cuntaramūrtti viḷakkavuriyuṭaṉ kallāṭam veḷivantatu.
1971 -il te.po.mī avarkaḷāl oṉpatu  piratikaḷik koṇṭu oppiṭṭu araciṉar kīḻtticic cuvaṭikaḷ nūlakap patippāka veḷivantatu. 2003 -il ti.vē. kōpāliyar, na. araṇamuṟuval ākiyōrip patippāciriyarākak koṇṭu tamiḻ maṇ patippakam kallāṭaṉār uriyip patippit tuḷḷatu. 2007 -il ti.vē. kōpāliyarip  patippāciriyarākak koṇṭu, caracuvati makāl nūlakam tolkāppiyam collatikāram urikkottil kallāṭar uriyi veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu. kallāṭar uri muḻumiyāka ituvari kiṭittilatu.
immīḷpatippil centamiḻ antaṇar avarkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa vāḻviyal viḷakkamum kā.  veḷḷivāraṇaṉāriṉ iyal viḷakkaṅkaḷum koṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivi tolkāppiyattiṟkuḷ pukuvārukku eḷitāṉa maṉaniliyi uruvākkuvatuṭaṉ, āyvāḷarkaḷukku mikunta payaṉ uṭiyatāka irukkum. piṉṉiṇippāka iṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷa kaṭṭurikaḷ, tolkāppiya uriyāciriyar, urikaḷ kuṟitta nuṭpattip pulappaṭuttuvatuṭaṉ, tolkāppiyam paṟṟiya nuṇmiyi aṟiyavum tuṇi ceyyum. nūṟpā, uri vaḻik kaliccoṟkaḷ, uriyil uḷḷa mēṟkōḷ akarāti pōṉṟavi tokuttut tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tolkāppiyatti aṟiya virumpuvārukkum, āyvāḷarkaḷukkum  it tokuppu  payaṉ koḷḷum vakiyil uruvākkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
tolkāppiyam mūlamum, uriyōṭum pala patippukaḷik kaṇṭuḷḷatu.  iruppiṉum toṭakkattil tamiḻ aṟiñar perumakkaḷāl uriyōṭu tolkāppiyattip patippitta patippukaḷ iṉṟu kiṭikkavilli. anniliyip pōkkum poruṭṭu, tamiḻariṉ paṇpāṭṭi, mēṉmiyi, uyarvip potittu vittuḷḷa nūlkaḷit tēṭit tēṭip patippikkum  tamiḻmaṇ patippaka urimiyāḷar aiyā kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār avarkaḷ paḻam patippukaḷip patippikka vēṇṭum eṉṟu virumpiyataṉ kāraṇamāka ippatippup paṇi mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭatu. ippatippiṟku, tamiḻiṉ mūtta aṟiñar, centamiḻ antaṇar mutumuṉivar aiyā irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷ patippāciriyarāka amintu eṉṉiyum ippaṇiyil iṇittukkoṇṭārkaḷ. 
 paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷip pātukāttu vittuḷḷa pulavar pallaṭam māṇikkam avarkaḷiṭam iruntu te.po. mīṉāṭci cuntaraṉār tolkāppiyam collatikāram kallāṭar uri peṟappaṭṭup patippiṟkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. maṟṟa urikaḷ aṉittum  tamiḻ iṇiyap palkalikkaḻaka nūlakam, ulakat tamiḻārāycci iṇiyap pakkattil iruntu pativiṟakkam ceyyappaṭṭu im mīḷpatippiṟkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  ci. kaṇēciyar patippiṉi ulakat tamiḻārāycci niṟuvaṉattiṉ vaḻi veḷivanta ci. kaṇēciyyariṉ tolkāppiyap patippiṟku ulakat tamiḻarāycci niṟuvaṉattiṉ mēṉāḷ iyakkunar (muḻukūṭutal poṟuppu) muṉivar tiru ma. irācēntiraṉ avarkaḷ kaṇēciyar patippiṟku eḻutiyuḷḷa mukavuri avaruṭiya opputaluṭaṉ ippatippil cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. 
muṉivar pā. matukēvaraṉ avarkaḷiṉ tolkāppiyam patippu āvaṇam 7 eṉṉum nūṟpakutiyil iruntu tol kāppiyap patippu aṭivukaḷ ippatippil iṇikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tolkāppiyam toṭarpāṉa palvēṟu aṟiñar  perumakkaḷiṉ āyvuk kaṭṭurikaḷ tokuttu vaḻaṅkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  tolkāppiyam urikaḷ muḻutum āyvu ceytuḷḷa pērāciriyar muṉivar ca. kurucāmi avarkaḷiṉ kaṭṭurikaḷ ippatippil iṇikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  patippiṟkut tuṇi ceyta mēṟkaṇṭa aṉivarukkum naṉṟiyiṉip pulappaṭuttik koḷḷukiṟēṉ. muṉivar srī. piracāntaṉ avarkaḷ tokutta   ilakkaṇa varampu nūlilirunta kaṭṭurikaḷ kaṇēciyar patippil iṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. centamiḻ, tamiḻppoḻil, tamiḻiyal mutaliya āyvitaḻkaḷiliruntu tolkāppiyam toṭarpāṉa kaṭṭurikaḷ cērkkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. 
patippup paṇiyil īṭupaṭuttiya aiyā tiru kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār avarkaḷukkum patippil iṇintu ceyalpaṭap paṇitta aiyā mutumuṉivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷukkum, patippiṟkut tuṇipurinta pulavar centali kutamaṉ aiyā avarkaḷukkum eṉ neñcam niṟinta naṉṟi. taṭṭaccuc ceytu koṭutta tirumati pa. kayalviḻi  tirumati kō. cittirā. aṭṭi vaṭivamitta celvaṉ pā.  ari (hariṣ),  maṟṟum patippakap paṇiyāḷarkaḷ tiru irā. paramēcuvaraṉ, tiru taṉacēkaraṉ, tiru ku. marutu,  tiru vi. matimāṟaṉ, ippatippiṟku utaviya eṉṉuṭiya muṉivar paṭṭa māṇavarkaḷ pā. mālati, kā. pāpu, cu. kōvintarācu, kā. kayalviḻi ākiya aṉivarukkum naṉṟiyiṉit terivittuk koḷkiṟēṉ. ip  patippup paṇiyil eṉṉi muḻuvatāka īṭupaṭat tuṇiyāka niṟkum eṉ kaṇavar maruttuvar mu. cēkkiḻār avarkaḷukku eṉ naṉṟiyiṉi urittākkukiṟēṉ.
kalpaṉā cēkkiḻār

nuḻivuri
tamiḻmaṇ patippakam itukāṟum veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa aṟiñar perumakkaḷiṉ urikaḷ yāvum paḻiya ilakkiya ilakkaṇak karuvūlattiṉ vāyilkaḷit tiṟakkiṉṟa tiṟavukōlkaḷ; tamiḻari ēṟṟiviṭum ēṇippaṭikaḷ; varalāṟṟup pātiyik kaṭakka utavum ūrtikaḷ.
tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷiṉ aṟivuccelvaṅkaḷi muḻu muḻu nūl tokuppukaḷāka veḷiyiṭṭut tamiḻnūl patippil perum paṅkaḷippi em patippakam ceytu varuvati ulakat tamiḻarkaḷ aṉivarum aṟivar.
tamiḻakam vēṟṟiṉattavar paṭiyeṭuppāl tākkuṇṭu atirntu nilikulintu tamiḻmakkaḷ tam marapukaḷi maṟantapōtellām paḻamperum ilakkiyac celvaṅkaḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭi viḷakkit tamiḻ marapi vāḻac ceyta perumi uriyāciriyarkaḷukku uṇṭu. 
 tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ nilitta vāḻviṟkum veṟṟikkum kāraṇamāka iruppavarkaḷ uriyāciriyarkaḷē āvar. ilakkaṇak koḷkikaḷi viḷakki moḻikku varampu kaṭṭi ilakkiyak karuttukaḷi viḷakki kālantōṟum paṇpāṭṭi vaḷarttu, tamiḻ iṉattiṟkut toṇṭu ceyta perumi uriyāciriyarkaḷiyē cārum.
ovvoru uriyāciriyarum tamiḻiṉam uṟaṅkik koṇṭiruntapōtu viḻitteḻuntu, eḻuccikkural koṭutta muṟpōkkuc cintaṉiyāḷarkaḷ. avarkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷa urikaḷ yāvum kālattiṉ kuralkaḷ; camutāyattiṉ etirpārppukaḷ; tamiḻ iṉa vaḷarccikku iṉṟiyamiyāta tēvikaḷ; nerukkaṭiyiṉ veḷippāṭukaḷ ākum.
	 - uriyāciriyarkaḷ, (mu.vi.  aravintaṉ).
tolkāppiyam ilakkaṇanūl maṭṭumaṉṟu; tamiḻariṉ aṟivumarapiṉ aṭiyāḷam.  tamiḻariṉ vāḻviyali, meyyiyalip pātukātta kālappēḻi. itil potintuḷḷa tarukkaviyal kūṟukaḷ intiyat tarukkaviyal varalāṟṟiṉ mūla vaṭivaṅkaḷ -  aṟiñarkaḷiṉ pārviyil pēraṟiñar aṇṇā, (muṉivar ka. neṭuñceḻiyaṉ)
mēṟkaṇṭa aṟiñarkaḷiṉ kūṟṟu, tolkāppiyattiṉ iṉṟiyami yāmiyiyum, uriyāciriyarkaḷiṉ karuttuc ceṟiviyum uḻippiyum uṇartta vallavi. ci.vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷi, kā. namaccivāya mutaliyār, va.u. citamparaṉār,  ci. kaṇēciyar, irā. vēṅkaṭācalam piḷḷi, te.po. mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉār pōṉṟa peruntamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷiṉ uḻippāl uruvāṉa iv vāḻviyal karuvūlam tolkāppiya  urittoki eṉum peyaril  tamiḻmaṇ patippakam mīḷ patippu ceytuḷḷatu. 
2003 -ām āṇṭil em patippakam tolkāppiyatti (eḻuttu - col - poruḷ)  muḻumiyāka veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu. itukāṟum veḷivantuḷḷa tolkāppiya nūl patippukaḷil  iṭam peṟāta ariya patippuc ceytikaḷ ivvurit tokiyil cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ivvurit toki ellā niliyilum ciṟappāka veḷivaruvataṟku  tōṉṟāt tuṇiyāka iruntavarkaḷip paṟṟi taṉippakkattil pativu ceytuḷḷōm.  
ivvāḻviyalnūl piḻiyaṟṟa cempatippāka veḷivaruvataṟku mutaṉmip patippāciriyar mutumuṉivar aiyā  irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār, iṇi patippāciriyar arumi makaḷ muṉivar kalpaṉā cēkkiḻār ākiyōriṉ uḻippum paṅkaḷippum eṉṟum maṟakka muṭiyātavi. em tamiḻ nūl patippiṟku ellā niliyilum toṭarntu ākkamum ūkkamum aḷittu varum iṉiya naṇpar pulavar centali na. kavutamaṉ avarkaḷiṉ taṉṉalam karutā tamiḻt toṇṭiṟku eṉṟum naṉṟi uṭiyēṉ.   
tamiḻarkaḷ tam illamtōṟum pātukāttu vikka vēṇṭiya ivvaruntamiḻp putiyalit tamiḻ kūṟum ulakiṟku vaḻaṅkuvatip perumiyākak karuti makiḻkiṟōm. ippatippit tamiḻkkūṟum nallulakam ēṟṟippōṟṟum eṉṟu nampukiṟōm.
kō.  iḷavaḻakaṉ

nūlākkattiṟku utaviyōr

mutumuṉivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār
iṇippatippāciriyar:	
muṉivar kalpaṉā cēkkiḻār
		
nūl vaṭivamippu:
tirumati. kayalviḻi, kō. cittirā
mēlaṭṭi vaṭivamippu: 
celvaṉ pā. ari (Harrish)

pulavar paṉaci aruṇā	pulavar mu. irācavēlu	muṉivar  aru. apirāmi	muṉivar jā. kiricā

poruḷatikāram  -	iḷampūraṇam-2
kaḷaviyal - kaṟpiyal -
poruḷiyal - meyppāṭṭiyal
va.u.citamparampiḷḷi	(1921, 1933, 1935)

vāviḷḷa irāmasvāmi cāstrulu aṇṭ saṉs, vāviḷḷa piras, ceṉṉi. (1933) il patippikkappaṭṭa nūli  mūlamākak koṇṭu ippatippu mīḷpatippāka veḷivarukiṟatu



kaḷaviyal
kaḷavoḻukkam uṇarttiṉamiyāl kaḷaviyaleṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. kaḷavāvatu aṉpu, aruḷ, aṟivu, aḻaku, kuṭippiṟappu mutaliyavaṟṟāl ottu viḷaṅkun talivaṉum taliviyum nallūḻiṉ ceyalāl tāmē etirppaṭṭu, ulakattāraṟiyātu maṟintoḻukutal. aimperum pātakaṅkaḷu ḷoṉṟākap pēcappaṭuṅ kaḷaveṉpatu piṟarkkuriya poruḷi vañcaṉiyāṟ kavarntukoḷḷutalākiya kuṟṟamākum. iḵtu attaṉmiyataṉṟi otta aṉpuṭiya kaṉṉiyari avartam icivaṟintu cuṟṟattāraṟiyātu kātalāl uḷaṅkalantu paḻakum peruṅkēṇmi yātalāl ciṟappuṭiya aṟameṉavē koḷḷappaṭum. otta aṉpuṭiya oruvaṉum oruttiyumākiya iruvarum tamatu neñcak kalappiṉip piṟaraṟiyātapaṭi ulakar muṉ muṟin toḻukiṉarākaliṉ, karantavuḷḷattarākiya avviruvaratu oḻukalāṟu, kaḷaveṉṟa collāl vaḻaṅkappaṭu vatāyiṟṟu. ikkaḷaviṉi maṟintavoḻukkam, maṟi, arumaṟi yeṉṟa coṟkaḷāl vaḻaṅkuvar āciriyar.
“mēl kikkiḷimutaṟ peruntiṇi iṟuvāyāka eḻu tiṇiyoti avaṟṟiṉ puṟattu nikaḻum tiṇikaḷum ōtippōn tār. avveḻutiṇiyiṉum orutalivēṭkiyākiya kikkiḷiyum oppil kūṭṭamākiya peruntiṇiyum oḻittu, iruvar aṉpum otta nilimiyākiya naṭuvaṇintiṇik kaṇṇum puṇarppum, piritalum iruttalum, iraṅkalum, ūṭalumākiya uripporuḷ, kaḷavu kaṟpu eṉṉum iruvakik kikōḷiṉum nikaḻumātaliṉ avviruvakik kikkōḷiṉuṅ kaḷavākiya kikōḷ ivvōttiṉuḷ uṇarttutalāṉ avaṟṟiṉpiṉ kūṟappaṭṭatu” eṉa muṉṉuḷḷa iyalkaḷōṭu ivviyalukkuḷḷa toṭarpiṉi viḷakkiṉār iḷampūraṇar.
“iṉpattiṟkup potuvilakkaṇam akattiṇiyiyaluṭ kūṟi, ataṟkiṉamākiya poruḷum aṟaṉum kūṟum puṟattiṇiyi ataṉ puṟattu nikaḻtaliṟ puṟattiṇiyiyaluṭkūṟi, īṇṭu avviṉpattiṉi virittuc ciṟappilakkaṇam kūṟutaliṉ, iḵtu akattiṇiyiyalōṭu iyipuṭittāyiṟṟu” eṉṟār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. ivviyaṟ cūttiraṅkaḷi 51-āka iḷampūraṇarum 50-āka nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum pakuttu urikūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
aṉpiṉintiṇiyākiya ik kaḷavoḻukkattiṉik kāmappuṇarcci, iṭantalippāṭu pāṅkoṭutaḻāal, tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam eṉa nālvakiyākap pakutturippar. aṉpuṭiyār iruvar muṟpiṟappiṉ nalviṉiyāl etirppaṭṭu oruvari yoruvar iṉṟiyamiyātavarāy aṉpiṉāl uḷamottalākiya neñcak kalappē kāmap puṇarcciyākum. iruvaratu uḷḷak kuṟippum muyaṟciyumiṉṟi nalviṉip payaṉākat taṉṉiyalpil nikaḻum ivvuṟaviṉi iyaṟkip puṇarcci, teyvappuṇarcciyeṉṟa peyarkaḷāl vaḻaṅkuvar muṉṉiyōr. iyaṟkip puṇarcci puṇarnta talimakaṉ piṟṟināḷum avviṭattiṟ ceṉṟu etirppaṭutal iṭantalippāṭākum. taliviyōṭu taṉakkuḷḷa uṟaviṉit talivaṉ taṉ uyirtōḻaṉākiya pāṅkaṉukkuc colli, nī eṉakkuttuṇiyāka vēṇṭumeṉa vēṇṭa, avaṉ kuṟivaḻic ceṉṟu talimakaḷ niṉṟa niliyi yuṇarntu vantuṇarttiyapiṉ ceṉṟu kūṭutal pāṅkaṟkūṭṭa meṉappaṭum. ikkaḷavoḻukkam nīṇṭa nāḷikkut toṭarntu nikaḻavēṇṭumeṉa virumpiya talivaṉ. talivikkuc ciṟantāḷākiya tōḻiyi irantu piṉṉiṉṟu avaḷ vāyilākat taliviyik kūṭal tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭamākum. ivi nāṉkum immuṟiyē nikaḻum.
iṉi, immuṟi nikaḻātu iṭiyīṭupaṭṭu varutalum uṇṭu. talimakaḷi yetirppaṭṭa kālattu aṉpuṭiyā rellārkkum iyaṟkippuṇarcci taṭiyiṉṟi nikaḻumeṉpataṟ killi. talimakaḷi yātāṉumōriṭattu etirppaṭṭa talimakaṉ, avaḷ kātaṟ kuṟippuṇarntu kūṭṭattiṟku iṭiyīṭu uṇṭāyavaḻi aṅkē ceṉṟa vēṭki taṇiyātu vantu, nēṟṟuk kaṇṭāṟpōl pōla iṉṟuṅ kāṇalākumō eṉa eṇṇi aṅkē maṟunāḷuñ cellutalum, talimakaḷum avvāṟē vēṭkiyāl aṭarppuṇṭu aṅkē varutalum ākiyavaḻip puṇarcci nikaḻum. aṅkē āyattārālō piṟarālō iṭiyīṭu paṭṭaviṭattut talivaṉ taṉ varuttattiṉip pāṅkaṉukku uṇartti, avaṉ talimakaḷ  niṉṟa niliyaṟintuvantukūṟa aṅkē ceṉṟum kūṭuvaṉ. av viṭattum iṭiyīṭupaṭiṉ tōḻivāyilāka muyaṉṟeytuvaṉ. iṉi oru kūṭṭamum nikaḻātu aṅkuṇṭākiya vēṭki iruvarkkun taṇiyātu niṉṟu maṇañceyta piṉṉark kūṭutalum uriyaṉ. ivvakiyiṉāl ikkaḷavoḻukkam mūvakip paṭumeṉṟār iyampūraṇar.
ellāvuyirkkum uriya iṉpavuṇarvum, avviṉpattiṟkuk kāraṇamākiya poruḷum, apporuḷiṉi yīṭṭutaṟkuriya varampākiya aṟamum eṉac collappaṭṭu aṉpiṉāl nikaḻum aintiṇikkaṇ nikaḻum kāmak kūṭṭattiṉi ārāyuṅkāl, maṟiyōriṭattu ōtappaṭṭa eṇvaki maṇattuḷḷum tuṟiyami nalyāḻat tuṇimiyōrākiya kantaruvaratu oḻukalaṟṟiṉi yokkum eṉpar āciriyar. kantaruvarāvār nalla yāḻamittu ici pāṭutalil vallavareṉṟum avartām ekkālattum āṇum peṇṇumāka iṇintē cellum iyalpiṉareṉṟum kūṟupavātaliṉ, avarkaḷit ‘tuṟiyami nalyāḻttuṇimi yōr’ eṉṟār tol kāppiyaṉār. avarkālattē vaṭavar nākarikam tamiḻakattil mella mellap paravat talippaṭṭamiyāl maṟiyōrtē ettu maṇamuṟikkum teṉṟamiḻnāṭṭu maṇamuṟikkumuḷḷa oṟṟumi vēṟṟumikaḷi viḷaṅkavuṇarttal avaratu kaṭaṉāyiṟṟu. oruvaṉ oruttiyeṉṉum iruvariṭiyē yuṇṭākum kūṭṭuṟavi orutalik kēṇmiyākiya kikkiḷi. otta kēṇmiyākiya aṉpiṉintiṇi ovvākkēṇmiyākiya peruntiṇiyeṉa mūvakiyākap pakutturittāl tamiḻ marapu. piramam, piracāpattiyam, āriṭam, teyvam, kāntaruvarum, acuram, irākkatam, picācam eṉa eṭṭuvakiyākap pakuttal vaṭanūl marapākum nāṟpatteṭṭāṇṭu piramacāriyaṅ kātta iḷiñaṉukkup paṉṉiraṇṭu vayatuṭiya kaṉṉiyi aṇikala ṉaṇintu koṭuppatu piramam. mittuṉa kōttirattāṉ makaḷ vēṇṭic ceṉṟāl maṟukkātu koṭuppatu piracāpattiyam. takutiyuṭiyāṉoruvaṉukkup poṉṉiṉāṟ pacuvum erutuñ ceytu avaṟṟiṭiyē peṇṇi niṟutti aṇikalaṉ aṇintu ‘nīṅkaḷum ivipōṟ polintu vāḻmiṉ’ eṉa nīrvārttuk koṭuppatu āriṭam. vēḷvi ceyta āciriyaṉukku vēḷvit tīmuṉ kaṉṉiyit takkiṇiyākak koṭuppatu teyvam. kantaruva kumārarum kaṉṉiyarum tammuḷ etirppaṭṭuk kūṭiṉāṟpōṉṟu oruvaṉum oruttiyum tāmē yetirppaṭṭuk kūṭuvatu kantaruvam. kollumiyalpuṭiya erutiṉi aṭakkiyavaṉ ippeṇṇi maṇattaṟkuriyaṉ, villēṟṟiṉāṉ ivaḷi maṇattaṟ kuriyavaṉ, iṉṉatoru poruḷ tantāṉ ivaḷi maṇattaṟkuriyaṉ eṉa ivvāṟu colli, colliyavaṇṇañ ceytāṟkup peṇṇik koṭuppatu acurameṉappaṭum. taṉṉāl virumpappaṭṭa peṇṇi, avaḷ viruppattiṟkum cuṟṟattār viruppattiṟkum māṟāka valitiṟ kavarntu celvatu irākkatam eṉappaṭum. mūttāḷ, tuyiṉṟāḷ kaḷittāḷ ākiya makaḷirik kūṭutal picācam eṉappaṭum. maṟiyōrkkuriya nūliṟ collappaṭṭa ivveṭṭu maṇaṅkaḷiyum muṟiyē aṟanili, oppu, poruḷkōḷ, teyvam, yāḻōr kūṭṭam, arumporuḷ viṉinili, irākkatam, pēynili eṉa moḻipeyarttu vaḻaṅkuvar tamiḻar. iviyeṭṭum vaṭamoḻiyāḷarkkē yuriyaṉa veṉpār ‘maṟiyōrtēettu maṉṟal eṭṭu’ eṉṟār tolkāppiyaṉār. eṉavē ivveṇvaki maṇamuṟikaḷum tamiḻarkkuriyaṉa alla eṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
maṟiyōrkkuriyavākac collappaṭṭa eṇvaki maṇattiṉuḷḷum acuram, irākkatam, picācam ākiya mūṉṟum, tamiḻar kūṟiya kikkiḷippāṟpaṭuvaṉa veṉṟum, piramam, piracāpattayam, āriṭam, teyvam eṉṟa nāṉkum peruntiṇiyāyaṭaṅkumeṉṟum, kantaruvameṉṟa oṉṟum aintiṇiyiṉpāṟpaṭumeṉṟum iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārk kiṉiyarum vakutturittuḷḷārkaḷ. aṉpoṭu puṇarnta aintiṇi maruṅkiṟ kāmakkūṭṭam, maṟiyōrtēettu maṉṟaleṭṭaṉuḷ tuṟiyami nalyāḻt tuṇimiyōrākiya kantaruvaratu iyalpiṉi yuṭiyateṉavē, kantaruva kumārarum kaṉṉiyarum tammuḷ etirppaṭṭuk kaṇṭu iyintāṟpōlat talivaṉum taliviyum tammuḷ etirppaṭṭup puṇarvatu ikkaḷavoḻukkamākumeṉak kantaruvattiṟkum kaḷavoḻuk kattiṟkumiṭiyē yaminta oṟṟumiyiṉi nacciṉārkkiṉiyar naṉku viḷakkiyuḷḷār.
oruvaṉum oruttiyumāka etirppaṭṭār iruvar, puṉalōṭum vaḻip puṟcāyntāṟpōlat tam nāṇamum niṟiyum iḻantu meyyuṟu puṇarcciyiṟ kūṭi makiḻum iyalpē kantaruva maṇamākum. iṅṅaṉam kūṭiṉōr tam vāḻnāḷ muḻutuṅ kūṭi vāḻvareṉṉum niyatiyilli. etirppaṭṭa aḷavil vēṭki mikutiyāṟ kūṭip piṉ aṉpiṉṟip pirintu māṟum varampaṟṟa niliyum ik kantaruvattiṟku uṇṭu. tamiḻar kūṟum kaḷavoḻukkamō iruvaruḷḷattum uḷniṉṟu curanta aṉpiṉ perukkiṉāl, tāṉ avaḷ eṉṉum vēṟṟumiyiṉṟi iruvarum oruvarāyoḻukum uḷḷap puṇarcciyēyākum. ivvuḷḷap puṇarcci nikaḻnta piṉṉar oruvariyoruvar iṉṟiyamiyā toḻukum uyirōraṉṉa ceyirtīr naṭpē cāntuṇiyum nilipeṟṟu vaḷarvatākum. uḷḷap puṇarcci nikaḻntapiṉ oruvariyoruvar piriviṉṟi yoḻukum aṉpiṉ tūṇṭutalāl ulakaṟiya maṇantu vāḻum kaṟpeṉṉun tiṇmi, tamiḻar oḻukalāṟākiya kaḷavoḻukkattiṉ muṭinta payaṉākum. ulakiyalil uḷavākum palavaki iṭiyūṟukaḷāl oruvari yoruvar maṇantukoḷḷa iyalāmal uḷḷappuṇarcci yaḷavē kūṭi vāḻntu piṉṉar iṟanta kātalarum ittamiḻakattu iruntaṉar. maṇimēkaliyiṟ kūṟappaṭum tarumatattaṉ vicāki yeṉṉum iruvarum yāḻōr maṇamākiya kantaruva muṟiyiṟ poruntiyavarkaḷ eṉat tammi nōkki ūrār kūṟiya paḻimoḻiyi vilakkit tam vāḻnāḷ muḻuvatum meyyuṟu puṇarcciyi virumpātu uḷḷap puṇarcciyaḷavil niṉṟu uyir tuṟanta varalāṟu iṅku niṉittaṟkuriyatākum.
kantaruva vaḻakkil meyyaṟu puṇarcci mutaṟkaṇ tōṟṟuvatu. ataṉ payiṟciyāl uḷḷap puṇarcci nilipeṟṟuc cākumaḷavum kūṭi vāḻtalum uṇṭu; uḷḷap puṇarcci tōṉṟātu tammetirppaṭṭārik kūṭi māṟutalum uṇṭu, eṉṟum piriyā niliyil niṉaṟa kaṭavātu aṉpiṉāṟ kūṭum uḷḷap puṇarcciyē kaḷavoḻukkattiṉ ciṟappiyalpākum. ituvē tamiḻiyal vaḻakkamākiya kaḷavukkum vaṭanūl maṇamākiya kantaruvattukkumuḷḷa uyir niliyāya vēṟupāṭākum. ‘aṉpoṭu puṇarnta aintiṇi maruṅkiṟ kāmakkūṭṭam’ eṉat tolkāppiyaṉārum ‘aṉpuṭi neñcan tāṅkalantaṉavē’ eṉap piṟacāṉṟōrum uḷḷap puṇarcci yoṉṟiyē kaḷavukkuriya ciṟappiyalpāka viritturittuḷḷārkaḷ. āriya maṇamākiya kantaruvattiṟkum tamiḻar oḻukalāṟākiya kaḷavoḻukkattiṟkum uḷḷa vēṟṟumiyiṉiyum tamiḻiyal vaḻakkameṉac ciṟappitturikkappaṭum kaḷavoḻukkattiṉ tūymiyiṉiyum āriya aracaṉ pirakattuṉukku aṟivikkum nōkkattuṭaṉ kapilar pāṭiya kuṟiñcippāṭṭu, kantaruvattiṟkum kaḷaviṟkumuḷḷa vēṟṟumiyiṉi iṉitu viḷakkuvatākum. innuṭpattiṉi naṉkuṇarntē ‘kantaruvarkkuk kaṟpiṉṟi yamiyavum peṟum, īṇṭuk kaṟpiṉṟik kaḷavē amiyātu” eṉa ivviraṇṭaṟku muḷḷa vēṟṟumiyiṉi viḷakkiṉār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
makaḷiri aḵṟiṇip poruḷākiya uṭimipōlak karutip piṟarpāṟ kēṭṭup peṟutalum koṭarāyiṉ cuṟṟat tārkkut terintō teriyāmalō vaṉmiyiṉāṟ kavarntu cēṟalumākiya ceyal muṟikaḷi maṇameṉak kūṟum vaḻakkam tamiḻarkkilli. ākavē ittakiya poruntā maṇa muṟi kaḷukku vaṭanūlkaḷiṟpōlat tamiḻ nūlkaḷil ilakkiyaṅ kāṇutalaritu. ‘aṉpoṭu puṇarnta aintiṇi’ yeṉavē ataṉ muṉṉumpiṉṉuṅ kūṟappaṭṭa orutalik kāmamākiya kikkiḷi yilum ovvāk kāmamākiya peruntiṇiyilum iruvarpālum otta aṉpiṉik kāṇutalariteṉpatu peṟappaṭum. ittakiya poruttamillāta kūṭṭuṟavukaḷ ennāṭṭilum ekkālattum kāṇappaṭuvaṉavēyām. poruntāta ceyalkaḷik kuṟippākac cuṭṭi vilakkiyum poruttamuṭiya naṟceyalkaḷi veḷippaṭiyāka eṭutturittu viḷakkiyum makkaḷi nalvaḻippaṭuttuvatē ciṟappuṭiya nūliṉ marapākum. immarapiṉi yuḷattuṭkoṇṭu kikkiḷi peruntiṇikaḷik kuṟippākavum aṉpiṉi ntiṇiyi virivākavum kūṟuvar āciriyar.
oruvaṉum oruttiyum kūṭi vāḻum maṉiyaṟattiṉa payaṉāka avviruriyum piṟapputtōṟum cērttu vippatum pirittu vilakkuvatumākiya iruvaki ūḻiṉum, iruvaruḷḷamum eṉkālattum oṉṟi vāḻtaṟkēṟṟa nallūḻiṉ āṇiyāl, otta aṉpuṭiya talivaṉum taliviyum tammuḷ etirppaṭṭuk kāṇpar. aṉpu mutaliyavaṟṟāl talivaṉ mikkavaṉāyiṉuṅ kuṟṟamilli.  icceyti,
“oṉṟē vēṟē yeṉṟiru pālvayiṉ
  oṉṟi yuyarnta pāla tāṇiyiṉ
  otta kiḻavaṉuṅ kiḻantiyuṅ kāṇpa
  mikkō ṉāyiṉuṅ kaṭivari yiṉṟē”
eṉa varum kaḷaviyaṟ cūttirattāluṇarappaṭum. iṅṅaṉam oruvaṉum oruttiyum etirppaṭum mutaṟ kāṭcikku nallūḻiṉ āṇiyē kāraṇameṉpār ‘uyarnta pāla tāṇiyiṉ’ eṉṟum, avvūḻiṉ āṇikkuk kāraṇamāvatu avviruvarum paṇṭip piṟappukkaḷiṟ payiliyatu keḻīiya naṭpeṉpār ‘oṉṟiyuyarnta pālatāṇi’ yeṉṟum, pala piṟavikaḷilum paḻakiya aṉpiṉ toṭarcciyē oruvariyoruvar iṉṟiyamiyātavarākakkāṇutaṟkuriya kātaṟ kiḻamiyi vaḻaṅkiyateṉpār, ‘ottakiḻavaṉuṅ kiḻattiyuṅkāṇpa’ veṉṟum kūṟiṉār tolkāppiyaṉār. talivaṉukkum talivikkum aminta otta paṇpukaḷāvaṉa: piṟappu, kuṭimi, āṇmi, āṇṭu, aḻaku, aṉpu, niṟi, aruḷ, uṇarvu, tiru eṉṉum ippattumākum. iyaṟkip puṇarccikkaṇ oruvari yoruvar etirppaṭṭaaḷavē vēṭkiyittūṇṭi niṟṟaṟkum avviruvarum paṇṭip piṟappiṟ payiliyatu keḻīiya naṭpaṉṟip piṟitu kāraṇamilliyeṉpatē tollāciriyar karuttākum. ōtta paruvattār oruvariyoruvar kaṇṭuḻi yellām puṇarcci vēṭki tōṟṟāmiyiṉ ‘oṉṟiyuyarnta pālatāṇiyiṟ kāṇpa’ eṉṟār āciriyar. īṇṭukkāṇutal eṉṟatu taṉakkuc ciṟantārākak karutali.
talivi otta nalaṅkaḷāṟ ciṟantu tōṉṟiyavaḻi ivaḷ makkaḷuḷḷāḷkollō teyvamō eṉat talivaṉuḷḷattē aiyam tōṉṟum oppumiyiṟ kuṟivuṭiyaḷāyiṉ avviḻipē avaḷi iṉṉāḷeṉat teḷivikkumātaliṉ, anniliyil, aiyantōṉṟutaṟkiṭamilli. talivi taṉṉiṉum uyarnta talivaṉi nōkki ivaṉ teyvamō makaṉō eṉa aiyuṟṟāl avaḷuḷḷattil accantōṉṟumēyaṉṟik kāmavuṇarvu tōṉṟātu. ākavē iṅṅaṉam aiyappaṭuvāṉ talimakaṉē yeṉpar. talimakaḷi nōkki ivaḷ teyvamakaḷō eṉat talivaṉ aiyuṟṟa kālattu, avaḷ kūntalilaṇinta māliyiṭattē moytta vaṇṭukaḷum avaḷaṇinta aṇikalaṉkaḷum naṟumalarum avaḷ pāl tōṉṟum taṭumāṟṟamum kaṇṇimippum accamum attaṉmip piṟavum aiyattiṉik kaḷitaṟkuriya karuviyākum. iṅkeṭuttuk kāṭṭiya aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷik koṇṭu talimakaḷi iṉṉāḷeṉat tuṇinta talimakaṉ, avaḷatu karuttaṟiyātu avaḷi yaṇukuvāṉāyiṉ, acceyal poruntā voḻukkamākiya peruntiṇiyāy muṭiyum otta aṉpiṉāl nikaḻtaṟkuriya ikkaḷavoḻukkattiṟkut talimakaḷatu uḷak karuttit talimakaṉ uṇarntu koḷḷutalē mutaṟkaṇ vēṇṭappaṭuvatākum. aṅkē oruvarōṭoruvar uriyāṭutalum muṟiyaṉṟām. oruvar vēṭkipōla maṟṟavarkkum vēṭkiyuḷatākuṅ kollō eṉa aiyuṟṟu niṉṟa iruvaratu aṟiviṉiyum oruppaṭuttaṟku avviruvar kaṇkaḷum vēṭkiyiṉāl oruvar oruvarkkurikkuṅ kāmakkuṟippuriyām, kaṇṭa aḷavilēyē vēṭkitōṉṟi oruvaratu uḷḷak kuṟippiṉi maṟṟavar ēṟṟukkoṇṭa niliyilētāṉ kaṇṇiṉāl varum ikkuṟippu nikaḻum.
taṉṉittāṉ koṇṭoḻukum perumiyum, ceṉṟa viṭaṅkaḷil maṉattic cellaviṭātu tīmiyiṉ nīkki naṉṟiṉ pāluykkum nallaṟivum āṭavaratu ciṟappiyalpākum. taṉatu niṟi kāvalukku iṭiyūṟu nērumō veṉṉum accamum, peṇṇiyalpākiya nāṇamum, tāṉ mēṟkoṇṭa koḷkiyi nekiḻaviṭāmiyākiya maṭaṉum makaḷiratu ciṟappiyalpākum. ivviru tiṟattārum tammik kāvātu vēṭki mītūrnta niliyiṟpuṉa lōṭum vaḻip puṟcāyntāṟpōlat tamakkuriya ikkuṇaṅkaḷi nekiḻaviṭutal kūṭāmiyiṉ, tām etirppaṭṭa mutaṟkāṭcilēyē meyyuṟu puṇarccikku uṭaṉpāṭatu uḷḷap puṇarcci yaḷavē yoḻuki maṇantukoṇṭa piṉṉarē kūṭutal muṟiyākum.
oruvariyoruvar peṟal vēṇṭumeṉṉum uḷḷa nikaḻcciyum iṭiviṭātu niṉittalum uṇṇāmiyāluḷa tām uṭal melivum, taṉakku ākkamāvaṉa iviyeṉat taṉak kuḷḷē collik koḷḷutalum, nāṇattiṉ elli kaṭatta lum, kāṇpaṉavellām aṉpuṭiyār uruvākavē tōṟṟu talum, tammi maṟattalum, maṉa mayakkamuṟutalum, uyir nīṅki ṉāṟpōṉṟu uyirp paṭaṅkutalum ākiya ivi yoṉpatum uyirōraṉṉa ceyirtīr naṭpākiya kāmavuṇarviṉic ciṟappip paṉavātaliṉ, ivaṟṟik kaḷavoḻukkattiṟkuc ciṟantaṉavākac kūṟuvar muṉṉiyōr. vēṭki mutal cākkāṭīṟāka iṅkuc collappaṭṭavi nikaḻnta piṉṉarē meyyuṟu puṇarcci nikaḻumeṉpar.
taṉiyiṭattē talimakaḷi yetirppaṭṭa talimakaṉ, taṉatu perumiyum uriṉum nīṅka vēṭki mītūrtalāl meyyuṟu puṇarcciyi virumpiṉāṉāyiṉum talimakaḷpāl tōṉṟum accamum nāṇum maṭaṉum ataṟkut taṭiyāy niṟpaṉavām. attaṭi nīṅkutaṟ poruṭṭut talimakaḷi muṉṉilippaṭuttic cila kūṟutalum, tāṉ collum colliṉvaḻi avaḷ niṟkum paṭi cila kūṟutalum, avaḷatu, nalattiṉi yeṭutturittalum, atu kēṭṭatalimakaḷpāl muṟuvaṟ kuṟipput tōṉṟi niṟṟaliyaṟitalum, taṉ akattē nikaḻum nōyāl puṟattu nikaḻum taḷarviṉik kuṟippāl viḷakkutalum, taṉṉuḷḷattu vēṭki mītūrtali nilippaṭac collutalum, talimakaḷ uḷḷap paṇpiṉit tāṉ aṟinta teḷiviṉit taṉ maṉattakattē tērntu veḷippaṭuttalum ākiya ivvuriyāṭalkaḷ talivaṉpāl nikaḻvaṉavām.
perumiyum uraṉumuṭiya talivaṉ, kātal veḷḷam puraṇṭōṭat taṉakkuc ciṟantāḷeṉat teḷinta taliviyiṉ meyyit tīṇṭip paḻakutalum, viḷiyāṭṭiṉkaṇ avaḷ nalattip pārāṭṭi yurittalum, avaḷ niṉṟa iṭatti neruṅki niṉṟu aṉpoṭu taḻīiya coṟkaḷic collutalum, talivaṉatu ūṟṟuṇarvu eṉṟum payilāta taṉ melliyal meyyiṟ paṭa nāṇamuṭiyaḷākiya talivi, aṅkuḷḷa kompum koṭiyumākiyavaṟṟit taṉakkuc cārpākak koṇṭu maṟintu olki niṟka, atukaṇṭa talivaṉ, ivvūṟṟiṉpattiṟku iṭiyūṟāy niṉ maṉattu nikaḻntavi yāviyeṉa viṉavi niṟṟalum, avaḷi meyyuṟutaṟkiyalum kālam vāykkāmiyi yeṇṇi varuntutalum, piṉṉar avaḷi meyyuṟutalum, mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuṭaṉ iṉpan tiḷittaliyum virivākap peṟṟaviṭattu, niṉṉip piriyēṉ eṉat talivikkut teḷivuri pakartalum ākiya eṭṭuvakik kūṟṟum, muṉkūṭṭam peṟṟa iṭattiṉiyē mīṇṭun talippaṭṭuk kūṭi makiḻtalum, talimakaḷip pirinta vaḻik kalakkamuṟutalum, eṉṟum nili niṟpatākiya illaṟatti mēṟkoḷḷa niṉintu mēl nikaḻvaṉavaṟṟi yeṭutturikku miṭattum taṉpāl cōrvu mikutiyālum kātal mikutiyālum nērvuṟṟa paḻipāvaṅkaḷi yeṭuttukkāṭṭi iṭitturikkum uyirt tōḻaṉākiya pāṅkaṉ taṉatu kaḷavoḻukkattiyēṟṟu uṭaṉpaṭṭaviṭattum talimakaḷāl virumpappaṭṭa uyirttōḻiyi vāyilākap peṟṟu avaḷi irantu piṉṉiṉṟu avaḷ kūṭṭak kūṭuvēṉ eṉak karuti avvirattali mēṟkoḷḷumiṭattum ūrum pērum tāṉ iḻantaṉa piṟavum ākiyavaṟṟi viṉavu mukattāl taṉ maṉak karuttup pulaṉākat tōḻiyik kuṟiyirantu niṟkum pakutiyum, tōḻi ivaṉ kūṟukiṉṟa kuṟi taliviyik kuṟittatāka viruntateṉṟu avaḷmēl cērtteṇṇum niliyiṟ cila kūṟalum, palakāluñ ceṉṟu irattalum maṟṟiyavaḻiyum talivaṉ varuntik kūṟukiṉṟa colliṉit taliviyoṭu cārttik kūṟutaliṉ muṉṉuṟu puṇarcci muṟiyēyaṭika veṉavum talivi pētit taṉmiyaḷ eṉavum ivvāṟu oḻukutalāṟ kēṭuḷatām eṉavum ivvoḻukkam niṉ perumikku ēlātu eṉavum kūṟit tōḻi talivaṉi avviṭattiṉiṉṟum añci nīkkutalāluḷatāya varutta nilimiyum nōkki maṭalēṟuvēṉ eṉak kūṟutalum ākiya kūṟṟukkaḷ talivaṉpāl nikaḻvaṉavām.
talimakaḷatu iḷamip paṇpiṉit tōḻi eṭuttuk kūṟit talivaṉ avviṭattiruntu peyartta vaḻiyum, varuttattatiṉāl melikiṉṟami kūṟiyaviṭattum, talimakaṉ kuṟiyi maṟukkun tōḻi aṉputōṉṟa nakitta niliyilum, avaḷatu uṭampāṭṭiṉippeṟṟu makiḻumiṭattum, tāṉ cellum vaḻiyiṭi iṭiyūṟuṇṭāyaviṭattum eṉattōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattiṭattē talivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻtal iyalpākum.
aṉpuṟṟāriruvar tuṇiyāyk kūṭutaṟku nimittamākavaṉa paṉṉiraṇṭām. aviyāvaṉa. kāṭci, aiyam, tuṇivu eṉa muṉṉark kūṟiya mūṉṟum, kuṟippaṟitaliṉ payaṉāyt tōṉṟum vēṭki mutal cākkāṭīṟākac collappaṭṭa oṉpatumākum. ivi paṉṉiraṇṭumē aṉpoṭu puṇarnta aintiṇimaruṅkiṟ kāmak kūṭṭattiṟku nimittamāvaṉavām. ivaṟṟuḷ muṟkūṟiya kāṭci, aiyam, tuṇivu eṉpaṉa mūṉṟum aṉpiṉintiṇikkuriya tātalēyaṉṟi orutalik kāmamākiya kikkiḷikkuriya kuṟippukkaḷākavum amiyum. nōkkuvavellām aviyē pōṟal, maṟattal, mayakkam, cākkāṭu eṉap piṉṉarkkūṟiya nāṉku nilikaḷum otta aṉpāl nikaḻum vaḻi aintiṇi yātalēyaṉṟi ovvāk kāmattāl nikaḻumvaḻip peruntiṇik kuriya poruntā nilikaḷākavum karutappeṟum. mutal, karu, uriyeṉṉun tiṇik kūṟupāṭṭuṭaṉ poruntiya yāḻōr neṟiyiṉiyotta kāmakkūṭṭam vēṭki, orutaliyuḷḷutal, melital, ākkañceppal, nāṇuvariyiṟattal eṉṉum aintu nilikaḷiyun taṉakkuriya ciṟappu nilikkaḷaṉākak koḷḷum eṉpar āciriyar.
tōḻiyiṉ uṭampāṭupeṟṟut talimakaḷik kūṭiya talimakaṉ avaḷi maṇantukoḷḷumvariyum kūṟum poruḷkaḷit tokuttuk kūṟuvatu ‘iruvakik kuṟipiḻippākiyaviṭattum’ eṉat toṭaṅkum ivviyaṟ cūttiramākum. itaṉit talivi kūṟṟākakkoṇṭu poruḷurippar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. taliviyiṉpāl nilipeṟṟuḷḷa nāṇamum maṭaṉum peṇmikku aṅka mātalāl kāmavoḻukkattiṉkaṇ kuṟippiṉālum iṭattiṉalu mallatu avaḷpāl vēṭki pulappaṭutalilli. vēṭki yuriyāta kaṇkaḷ yāṇṭumiṉmiyāl vēṭki kāraṇamāka accam nīṅkiṉālum nāṇamum maṭaṉumākiya iraṇṭum taliviyiṭattu eṉṟum nīṅkātuḷavām. talivaṉ puṇarcci karutik kūṟuñcolliṉ etirē icivillātārippōṉṟu kūṟutalē talivikku iyalpākum. iṉṉiṉṉa iṭaṅkaḷil talivi uriyāṭutaṟkuriyaḷ eṉpati ivviyal 21-ām cūttirattāl āciriyar virittuk kūṟiyuḷḷār.
iyaṟkip puṇarcci puṇarntu nīṅkiya taviṉ iṉṉa nāḷil niṉṉi maṇantukoḷvēṉ eṉac colliviṭṭut tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattiṟku muyalātu nīṅkiya niliyil, taṉṉik kaṇṭu tōḻi aiyuṟutaṟkēṟṟa kuṟippu taṉkaṇ tōṉṟātapaṭi maṟittoḻukiya talivi, talivaṉ varumaḷavum āṟṟātu varutta muṟṟa niliyilum, kaḷavoḻukkattil vantoḻukun talivaṉ cevilittāy mutaliyōri etirppaṭṭapoḻutum, ivvoḻukkattiṉi niṉ tōḻikkuc colluka eṉa avaṉ taṉakkuc colliya niliyilum tāṉē tōḻikkuk kūṟutaluṇṭu. uyirikkāṭṭilum nāṇam ciṟantatu; attakiya nāṇattiṉuṅ kaṟpuc ciṟappuṭiyatu eṉṉum muṉṉōr moḻiyi yuḷattuṭkoṇṭu, talivaṉ uḷḷaviṭattiyaṭiya niṉittalum ivvāṟu kuṟṟantīrnta naṟcoṟkaḷik kūṟutalumākiya ivi, talivi tāṉēkūṟuṅ kūṟṟiṉuḷ amitaṟkuriya poruḷvakiyeṉpar āciriyar.
kaḷavoḻukkattiṉkaṇ talivikku iṉṟiyamiyāta vaḷākiya tōḻi uriyāṭutaṟkuriya poruḷvaki yellā vaṟṟiyun tokuttuk kūṟuvatu, ‘nāṟṟamum tōṟṟamum (kaḷavu-24) eṉat toṭaṅkuñ cūttiramākum. itaṉkaṇ tōḻi kūṟutaṟkuriyaṉavāka muppattiraṇṭu poruṇmikaḷ virit turikkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. iṅkē tōḻi kūṟuvaṉavāka āciriyar eṭuttōtip poruṭpakutikaḷi uṟṟunōkkuṅkāl, makkaḷ maṉa niliyi avartam tōṟṟam oḻukkamutaliyavaṟṟāl uyt tuṇarum maṉappayiṟciyum otta aṉpiṉarākiya talivaṉiyum taliviyiyum ulakiyal kūṟit tītorīi naṉṟiṉpā luykkum nallaṟivum, uḷḷak karuttaṟitalarumiyum, taṉ aṟiviṉ tiṟattip piṟaraṟiyātu oḻukum aṭakkamum, maṟipulappaṭāmal niṟuttum nalluḷḷamum, mācaṟṟa aṟavuṇarcciyum, ceyyat takuvaṉa aṟintu kūṟalum ākiya peṇmikkuṇaṅkaḷ muḻuvatum tōḻiyiṉiṭattu oruṅkamittiruttal pulaṉām. ivvāṟu poṟuttaṟkariya peruṅ kuṇaṅkaḷ yāvum oruṅku vāykkap peṟṟavaḷē tōḻiyeṉap pārāṭṭutaṟkuriyava ḷeṉpār, ‘tāṅkaruñciṟappiṉ tōḻi’ eṉṟār tol kāppiyaṉār.
talivaṉatu kaḷavoḻukkam puṟattārkkup pulaṉākiya niliyil ūrār alar kūṟiya kālattum, taliviyiṉ vēṭki aḷaviṟanta niliyilum, avaḷatu vaṉappumikkut tōṉṟiya kālattum, talivaṉoṭu taliviyi orucērak kaṇṭa kālattum taliviyiṉ melivukkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷik kaṭṭuvippittum kaḻaṅku pārttum kaṇṭaṟintu avaḷatu nōytīra vēlaṉiyaḻittu veṟiyāṭiya poḻutum, veṟiyāṭutalit tōḻi taṭuttu niṟuttiya niliyilum, kātal mikutiyāl talivaṉi niṉintu talivi kaṉavil araṟṟiya poḻutum cevilittāy tōḻiyi viṉavutaluṇṭu. iṉṉavāṟu nikaḻntateṉat tōḻi kūṟiyavaḻi ivviruvaratu oḻukalāṟṟiṉāl kuṭikkup paḻi viḷitalākāteṉat teyvatti vēṇṭutalum talivi talivaṉuṭaṉ pōyiṉāḷeṉṟaṟinta niliyil tōḻiyoṭu ārāyntu avviruvariyum maṉiyaṟattiṉkaṇ niṟuttaṟku muyalutalum avaḷ ceyal. talivaṉ maṇantukoḷḷātu talimakaḷip pirinta kālattu avaḷ kaṟpuvaḻippaṭṭu maṉikkaṇ amitiyāka aṭaṅkiyirukkum niliyiṉi yeṇṇiya niliyilum talivaṉatu kuṭippiṟappu tam kuṭippiṟappiṉōṭu okkum eṉa ārāytaṟkaṇṇum ivipōṉṟa piṟaviṭaṅkaḷilum cevili uriyāṭutaṟkuriyaḷ. ceviliyuṇarvuṭaṉ otta karuttuṭiyaḷāyiṉ naṟṟāykkum mēṟcollappaṭṭa kūṟṟukkaḷ uriyaṉavām.
taliviyiṉ meliviṉik kaṇṭu varuntiya ceviliyum naṟṟāyum, kuṟṟamaṟṟa ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya uyarntōrākiya aṟivarip paṇintu niṉṟu ivaḷatu melivu etaṉālāyiṟṟu eṉa viṉavi niṟpar. atu kēṭṭa periyōr, mukkāla nikaḻcciyiṉiyum tām oruṅkuṇarum nuṇṇuṇarvuṭi yōrātaliṉ, taliviyoṭu talivaṉukkuṇṭākiya toṭarpiṉi veḷippaṭac collutal marapaṉmiyāṉum nikaḻntati maṟittal vāymikku māṟātalāṉum ‘nummakaḷ talimakaṉ aṟiyā aṟiviṉiyuṭiyāḷ’ eṉa aiyak kiḷaviyāl maṟumoḻi kūṟuvar. etirkālattil taṉṉi maṇattaṟkuriya kaṇavaṉālum aṟiyappaṭāta pēraṟiviṉiyuṭiyāḷ nummakaḷ eṉa oru poruḷum, taṉ kaṇavaṉākiyoḻukum talivaṉālum aṟiyappaṭāta aṟivurimipūṇṭu mayaṅkukiṉṟāḷ. ivaḷ taṉṉaṟiviṟ ciṟitum mayakkamilaḷ, avaṉ poruṭṭu mayaṅkukiṉṟāḷ eṉa maṟṟoru poruḷum tarum niliyil iṅṅaṉam aiyuṟakkūṟiya aṟivaratu coṟporuḷi yuyttuṇarntu talivaṉ taliviyākiya iruvariṭiyē yaminta toṭarpiṉic ceviliyum naṟṟāyum aṟintukoḷḷutalum uṇṭu.
vēṟu vēṟākat tammuḷ kātal ceytoḻukum aṟivillā tārippōlat taṉatu mikka vēṭkiyit talivaṉ muṉpu colluñ col, taliviyiṭattu nikaḻtalilli. aṅṅaṉam col nikaḻātoḻiyiṉum taliviyatu vēṭki putukkalattup peytanīr puṟattē pocintu kāṭṭumāṟupōlum uṇarviṉi yuṭitteṉṟu kūṟuvar. iyaṟkip puṇarcciyākiya kaḷavu, kūṭṭivippār piṟariṉṟit taṉimiyiṟ polivatu. ātaliṉ talivaṉ talivi yiruvarum tattamatu uḷakkaruttip pulappaṭuttun tūtuvarākat tāmē niṉṟu kūṭutalum uṇṭu annili meyppāṭṭiyaluḷ ‘pukumukam purital’ mutaliya meyppāṭu paṉṉiraṇṭāṉum naṉkaṟiyappaṭum. eṉavē talivaṉ pāṅkaṉatu utavipeṟṟuk kūṭutalum tōḻiyiṉ uṭaṉpāṭu peṟṟuk kūṭutalumākiya ivi yāvar māṭṭum nikaḻavēṇṭumeṉṉum variyaṟiyilli yeṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉ piṉṉart talivaṉatu colliṉ elliyik kaṭattal talimakaḷukku aṟaṉaṉṟākalāṉum tāṉ cellutaṟkuriya iṭattit tāṉē yuṇarvaḷātalāṉum tāṅkaḷ mīṇṭum kaṇṭu aḷavaḷāvutaṟkuriya ōriṭatti yaṟivikkum poṟuppu talimakaḷic cārntatākum. tōḻiyāl aṟivikkappaṭṭup poruntumiṭamum uṇṭu.
kaḷaviṟpuṇarcci tōḻiyiṉ tuṇiyiṉṟi mūṉṟu nāḷikkumēl nikaḻtalilli. ammūṉṟu nāḷikkuḷḷum tōḻiyiṉtuṇi vilakkappaṭutalilliyeṉpar āciriyar. eṉavē iyaṟkip puṇarcci, iṭantalippāṭu, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam eṉṉum mūṉṟunāḷelliyaḷavum talivaṉ tōḻiyiṉ utaviyiṉṟi avaḷaṟiyātu talimakaḷikkaṇṭu aḷavaḷāvutal kūṭumeṉpatum, ammūṉṟu nāḷikkumēlāyiṉ tōḻiyiṉ utaviyiṉṟit talivi etirppaṭutaṟku ariyaḷeṉpatum, ammūṉṟu nāḷikkuḷḷēyē tōḻiyiṉ uṭaṉpāṭṭip peṟutalumuṇṭeṉpatum āciriyar karuttātal uyttuṇarap paṭum.
palavakiyāṉum talimakaḷpāluḷavām naṉmikaḷi nāṭuvār pakkattiṉi ārāyum ārāycci talivaṉukku vēṇṭu mātalāṉum, tāṅkaḷ mēṟkoṇṭa aṉpiṉ vaḻippaṭṭa kaḷavoḻukkam oṉṟiya aṉpuṭiya tuṇiyākiya tōḻiyāl puṟattāraṟiyātu nilipeṟṟu nikaḻtal vēṇṭumātalāṉum, taṉakkut tuṇiyāvāḷ iṉṉāḷeṉat talivaṉukkuc cuṭṭik kūṟuñ col talivikkuriyatākum eṉpar āciriyar.
kaḷavoḻukkattil naṇukiyārāyntaṟitaṟkariya maṟip poruḷellāvaṟṟiyum kēṭṭaṟkum collutaṟkum uriya tāyākac ciṟappitturikkappaṭupavaḷ ceviliyēyām. taṉakku iṉṟiyamiyāta uyirttōḻiyākat taliviyāl virumpappaṭṭa tōḻiyeṉpāḷ mēṟkūṟiya ciṟappuṭiya ceviliyiṉ makaḷāvāḷ. taliviyiṉ kaḷavoḻukkampaṟṟit taṉ maṉattuḷḷē ārāytalum taliviyiṉ cūḻccikkut tāṉ ucāttuṇiyāki niṟṟalumākiya peruṅkēṇmiyuṭiyāḷ it tōḻiyēyeṉpar.
talivaṉ taṉpālvantu kuṟiyuṟṟu niṟka avaṉuḷḷak karuttiṉi yuṇartalum, taliviyiṉ uḷḷak kuṟippiṉik kaṇṭuṇartalum, taliviyum tāṉum oruṅkirunta niliyil talivaṉvara anniliyil avviruvaratu uḷḷakkaruttiṉi yuṇartalum eṉat tōḻi, taṉ karuttuṭaṉ avviruvaratu karuttiṉiyum vittu oṉṟupaṭuttuṇarum uṇarcci mūvakippaṭum. ivvāṟu mūvar matiyiṉiyum oṉṟupaṭut tuṇartaliṉ itu matiyuṭampaṭuttaleṉṟu peyarāyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam talivaṉ taliviyeṉṉum iruvarpālum otta aṉpuṭimiyuṇarntapiṉallatu talivaṉ taṉṉi irantu piṉṉiṟkum muyaṟcikkut tōḻi iṭantaramāṭṭāḷ. taṉṉi irantu piṉṉiṟkun talivaṉatu niṉiviṉkaṭpaṭum mācaṟṟa aṉpiṉ tiṟattiyuṇarntu avaṉit taliviyōṭu kūṭṭuvittalum attōḻiyiṉ ceyalēyām.
talivaṉ talivi yiruvarum piṟaraṟiyātu pakalilum iravilum aḷavaḷāvutaṟkeṉak kuṟikkappaṭṭa iṭamē kuṟiyeṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭum. maṉiyuṉuṭpukātu aṅkuḷḷōr kūṟuñcoṟkaḷ kēṭkum aṇimikkaṇ amivatu iravukkuṟiyākum. ūriṉ matiṟpuṟamāyt talimakaḷ aṟintu cērtaṟkut takutiyuṭiyatākiya iṭam pakaṟkuṟiyākum. talivaṉ ceyta aṭiyāḷameṉa mayaṅtaṟkuriyaṉa iyaṟkiyāka nikaḻiṉ kuṟiyallātavaṟṟik kuṟiyeṉak koḷḷutalum talimakaḷukkuriya iyalpākum. kaṟpuṭi makaḷirkkuriya ciṟappiṟ kuṉṟāvāṟu mēṟkūṟiya iruvakik kuṟiyiṭaṅkaḷilum piṟaraṟiyātapaṭi talivaṉoṭukūṭi yoḻukum oḻukkamum talimakaḷukku uṇṭu.
iṅṅaṉam kaḷavoḻukkattil vantoḻukutal kāraṇamāka eṉṟum taṉ tōḻarkaḷuṭaṉ kalantukoḷḷutaṟ kuriya viḷiyāṭṭiṉiyum tiruviḻāc ceyalmuṟikaḷiyum vilakiyoḻukum oḻukkam talivaṉukkilli. yāvarālum virumpi nōkkappaṭun talivaṉ kaḷavoḻukkam kāraṇamāka ivaṟṟi vilakiyoḻuku vāṉāyiṉ avaṉ vāramipaṟṟi avaṉip palarum viṉava, atu kāraṇamāka avaṉatu kaḷavoḻukkam puṟattārkkup pulaṉāmātaliṉ, ivaṟṟi nīṅkiyoḻukutal kūṭāteṉa vilakkuvar āciriyar. ivvāṟē vaḻiyarumiyum neñcaḻitalum añcutalum iṭiyūṟum ākiya ivi talivaṉpāl nikaḻtalkūṭāteṉpar.
taliviyiṉ tantiyum tamiyaṉmārum ikkaḷavoḻuk kattik kuṟippiṉāl uṇarvar. naṟṟāy, ceviliyuṇarum muṟimiyāl aṟintukoḷvāḷ. iraviṉum pakaliṉum avvaḻi vantu cellun talimakaṉiyaṟintu avaṉatu varuki kāraṇamāka makaḷir cilarum palarum tammukak kuṟippiṉāṟ pulappaṭuttum ampalum, iṉṉāṉōṭu iṉṉāḷiṭiyatu naṭpu eṉac collāl viritturippatāya alarum tōṉṟiya piṉṉarallatu immaṟi veḷippaṭātātaliṉ, ikkaḷavu veḷippaṭutaṟkut talivaṉē kāraṇamāvaṉ, kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭa piṉṉart taliviyi maṇantukoḷḷutalum atu veḷippaṭutaṟku muṉṉarē maṇam ceytukoḷḷutalum eṉa maṇantukoḷḷum muṟi irutiṟappaṭum.
kaḷavu veḷippāṭē kaṟpiyal vāḻkkiyākak karutap paṭumāyiṉum ulakattāraṟiyat taliviyi maṇantukoḷḷāta niliyil ōtal, paki, tūtu eṉpaṉa kāraṇamākat talivaṉ neṭṭiṭip pirintu cēṟal kūṭāteṉpar āciriyar.
- ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉār
nūl varici -10, pak. 257-273



mūṉṟāvatu 
kaḷaviyal
89.	iṉpamum poruḷu maṟaṉu meṉṟāṅ
	kaṉpoṭu puṇarnta vintiṇi maruṅkiṟ
	kāmak kūṭṭaṅ kāṇuṅ kāli
	maṟiyōr tēettu maṉṟa leṭṭaṉuṭ
	ṭuṟiyami nalyāḻt tuṇimiyō riyalpē.
eṉpatu cūttiram
ivvōttu eṉṉa peyarttō eṉiṉ, kaḷaviyal eṉṉum peyarttu. kaḷavoḻukkam uṇarttiṉamiyāṟ peṟṟa peyar. aḵ tātal īṇṭu urikkiṉṟataṉāṉ payaṉ iṉṟām; kaḷaveṉpatu aṟam aṉmiyiṉ (eṉil), aṟṟaṉṟu; kaḷavu eṉṉum coṟ kaṇṭuḻi yellām aṟappāṟpaṭāteṉṟal amiyātu. kaḷavāvatu, piṟarkkuriya poruḷ maṟiyiṟ kōṭal. aṉṉataṉṟi, ottārkkum mikkārkkum potuvākiya kaṉṉiyarittamar koṭuppak koḷḷātu, kaṉṉiyar, tam icciyiṉāṉ tamari maṟittup puṇarntu piṉṉum 1aṟanili vaḻā maṉṟalāl, iḵtu 2aṟameṉappaṭum. aṉṉatātal iccūttirattāṉum viḷaṅkum.
aḵtaṟṟāka, mēli yōttiṉōṭu ivvōttiṟku iyipu eṉṉi yeṉiṉ, mēl kikkiḷi mutaṟ peruntiṇi iṟu vāyāka eḻutiṇi yōti avaṟṟiṉ puṟattu nikaḻun tiṇikaḷum ōtippōntār. avveḻutiṇiyiṉum orutali vēṭki yākiya kikkiḷiyum, oppilkūṭṭamākiya peruntiṇiyum oḻittu, iruvaraṉpum otta nilimiyākiya naṭuvaṇ aintiṇik kaṇṇum puṇarppum piritalum iruttalum iraṅkalum ūṭalumākiya uripporuḷ kaḷavu kaṟpu eṉṉum iruvakik kikōḷiṉum nikaḻumātaliṉ, avviruvakik kikōḷi ṉuṅ kaḷavākiya kikōḷ iv vōttiṉuḷ uṇarttutalāṉ avaṟṟiṉ piṉ kūṟappaṭṭatu. itu naṭuvaṇintiṇikkaṇ nikaḻum poruṭpākupāṭāyiṉ, akattiṇi yiyaliṉ piṉ vikkaṟpālatu eṉiṉ, āṇṭu vikkak karutiṉ ``veṭci tāṉē kuṟiñciyatu puṟaṉē’’ (puṟat. 59) eṉṉum māṭṭēṟu peṟātām, ataṉiṭik kaḷaviyalum kaṟpiyalum kiṭattalāṉ eṉka.
maṟṟum, aḵtu yāṅkaṉam uṇarttiṉārō eṉiṉ,
``kāmap puṇarcciyum iṭantalip paṭalum
pāṅkoṭu taḻāalun tōḻiyiṟ puṇarvumeṉ
ṟāṅkanāl vakiyiṉu maṟinta cārvoṭu
maṟiyeṉa moḻitaṉ maṟiyō rāṟē’’	(cey. 178)
eṉpataṉāṉ innālvakiyum itaṉuḷ urikkappaṭu kiṉṟateṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum. kāmappuṇarcciyeṉiṉum, iyaṟkip puṇarcci yeṉiṉum, muṉṉuṟu puṇarcci yeṉiṉum, teyvap puṇarcci yeṉiṉum okkum. iviyellām kāraṇappeyar. aḵtāvatu, ottār iruvar tāmē kūṭuṅ kūṭṭam. iṭantalippāṭāvatu, iyaṟkip puṇarcci puṇarnta talimakaṉ piṟṟiñāṉṟum av viṭattuc ceṉṟu etirp paṭutal. pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭamāvatu, ip puṇarcci pāṅkaṟku urittu, nī yemakkuttuṇiyāka 3vēṇṭumeṉa avaṉ kuṟivaḻic ceṉṟu talimakaḷ niṉṟa niliyi yuṇarttac ceṉṟu kūṭutal. tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭamāvatu, ivaṟṟiṉ piṉṉar ikkūṭṭam nīṭaccēṟal vēṇṭit tōḻiyi irantu piṉṉiṉṟu avaḷ vāyilākak kūṭutal. ivi nāṉkum immuṟiyē nikaḻum eṉṟu koḷka. iṉi immuṟi nikaḻātu iṭiyīṭu paṭṭum varum. aḵtāmāṟu: talimakaḷ etirppaṭṭuḻi, aṉpuṭiyār ellārkkum iyaṟkip puṇarcci muṭṭiṉṟik kūṭutal ulakiyal aṉmiyāṉ, talimakaḷi yātāṉum ōriṭattu etirppaṭṭa talimakaṉ avaḷ kātaṟ kuṟippuṇarntu niṉṟu, kūṭṭattiṟku iṭiyīṭu uṇṭāyuḻiyum āṇṭuc ceṉṟa vēṭki taṇiyātu niṉṟu, muṉṉi ñāṉṟu kaṇṭāṟ pōlap piṟṟi ñāṉṟum kāṇalākumō eṉa āṇṭuc cēṟalum, talimakaḷum avvāṟē vēṭkiyāṉ aṭarppuṇṭu āṇṭu varutalum ākiyavaḻip puṇarcci nikaḻum. āṇṭu āyattārāṉātal piṟarāṉātal iṭiyīṭupaṭṭuḻittaṉvaruttattiṉip pāṅkaṟku uṇartti, avaṉ talimakaḷ niṉṟuḻi yaṟintu kūṟa, āṇṭuc ceṉṟu puṇarum. avviṭattum iṭiyīṭu paṭṭuḻit tōḻi vāyilāka muyaṉ ṟeytum. ivvāṟum orōvoṉṟu iṭiyīṭu paṭṭuḻi varin teytal takkataṉṟō eṉiṉ, varinteytun tiṟam nīṭṭikkumāyiṉ vēṭki niṟutta lāṟṟātār puṇarcci karuti muyalpa. ivvāṟu cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷ vantaṉavum uḷavō eṉiṉ, cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷkaḷuḷ ivvāṟu poruḷkoḷḷa ēṟpaṉa uḷa. aviyāvaṉa:
``maruntiṟ ṟīrā maṇṇi  ṉākā
taruntava muyaṟciyi ṉakaṟṟalu  maritē
tāṉcey nōykkut tāṉmarun tākiya
tēṉimir naṟaviṉ ṟēṟal pōla
nītara vanta niṟiyarun tuyaraniṉ
ṉāṭukoṭi maruṅki ṉaruḷi  ṉallatu
piṟitiṟ ṟīrā teṉpatu piṉṉiṉ
ṟaṟiyak kūṟutu meḻumō neñcē
nāṭuvaḷaṅ koṇṭu pukaḻnaṭutal vēṇṭitta
ṉāṭumaḻit taṭakki yaṟuttumuṟi ceyta
poṟki naṟuntārp puṉitērp pāṇṭiyaṉ
koṟkiyam peruntuṟi kuṉitiri tokutta
viḷaṅkumut  tuṟikkum veṇpaṟ
paṉmāṇ cāyaṟ paratavar makaṭkē.’’	
itaṉuḷ ``āṭukoṭi maruṅki ṉaruḷiṉ ṉallatu piṟitiṟ ṟīrātu’’ eṉpataṉāṉ iyaṟkip puṇarcci iṭiyīṭupaṭṭup puṇarcci karutik kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka.
``mayilkoṉ maṭavāḷkoṉ mānīrt tiriyuṭ
payilvatōr teyvaṅkol kēḷir  kuyilpayilum
kaṉṉi yiḷañāḻaṟ pūmpoḻili ṉōkkiya
kaṇṇiṉ varuntumeṉ ṉeñcu.’’	(tiṇimoḻi. 49)
itaṉuḷ aiyanilip pāṅkaṟku urittaliṉ puṇarcci iṉṟāyiṟṟu.
``koṇṭaṉ māmaḻi 4kuṭakkērpu kuḻitta
ciṟukō liṇara peruntaṇ cāntam
vakicē rimpā ṟakipeṟa vārip
pularviṭat tutirtta tukaḷpaṭu kūḻip
peruṅka ṇāya muvappat tanti
neṭuntēr vaḻaṅku nilavumaṇal muṟṟattup
pantoṭu peyarum parivi lāṭṭi
yaruḷiṉu maruḷā ḷāyiṉum peritaḻintu
piṉṉili muṉiyaṉmā ṉeñcē yeṉṉatūu
maruntuya  ravalan tīrkku
maruntu piṟitilliyā  ṉuṟṟa nōykkē.’’	(naṟ. 140)
itaṉuḷ ‘aruḷiṉum aruḷāḷāyiṉum’ eṉṟamiyāṉ kūṭṭa miṉmiyum, ‘piṉṉili muṉiyal’ eṉṟamiyāṉ irantu piṉṉiṟpāṉākat 5tuṇintamiyum, tōḻiyiṟkūṭṭattu iyaṟkip puṇarccikku oruppaṭṭamiyum uṇarka.
``naṟavukkama  ḻalari naṟavu vāyvirin
tiṟaṅkitaḻ kamaḻu micivāy neytaṟ
kaṇṇit taliyar karuṅkip paratavar
niṉṉiya  rallarō neṟitā  ḻōti
yoṇcuṇaṅ kiḷamuli  yoruñāṉṟu puṇari
ṉuṇkayiṟ ṟuṟuvali numaroṭu vāṅkik
kiti vēli yivvūrc
ceytūṭ ṭēṉō ciṟukuṭi yāṉē.’’
periyīreṉac cēṭpaṭuttavaḻik kūṟiyatu.
``kūṟuvaṅ kollō kūṟalaṅ kolleṉak
karanta kāmaṅ kinniṟuk kallātu
nayantunām viṭṭa naṉmoḻi nampi
yarināḷ yāmattu viḻumaḻi karantu
kārmalar kamaḻuṅ kūntaṟ 6ṟūviṉi
nuṇṇū lākam poruntiṉaḷ veṟpi
ṉiḷamaḻi cūḻnta maṭamayil pōla
vaṇṭuvaḻip paṭarat  taṇmalar vēyntu
vilvakup puṟṟa nalvāṅku kuṭaccū
lañcilam poṭukki yañciṉaḷ vantu
tuñci yāmattu muyaṅkiṉaḷ peyarvō
ḷāṉṟa kaṟpiṟ cāṉṟa pēriya
lammā variviyō vallaḷ 7teṉāa
tāay naṉṉāṭ  ṭaṇaṅkuṭic cilampiṟ
kaviram peyariya vurukeḻu kavāa
ṉērmali niṟicuṉi yuṟiyuñ
cūrmakaḷ kolleṉat tuṇiyumeṉ ṉeñcē.’’ 	(akam. 198)
itu tōḻiyiṟkūṭiya talimakaṉ kūṟṟu.
``avari poruntiya piṅkura lēṉaṟ
kavari maṭamā katūum paṭarcāraṉ
kāṉaka nāṭa maṟaval vayaṅkiḻik
kiyāṉiṭi niṉṟa puṇi.’’	(aintiṇiyeḻu. 1)
itaṉāṉē muntuṟṟa kūṭṭamiṉmi yuṇarka. iṉi oru kūṭṭamum nikaḻātu āṇṭu vantaṭivēṭki iruvarkkum taṇiyātu niṉṟu varinteytalum oṉṟu. ivvakiyiṉāṉ ikkaḷavoḻukkam mūvakippaṭum.
iṉi, ittaliccūttiram eṉṉutaliṟṟō veṉiṉ, kāmap puṇarccikku ilakkaṇa vakiyāṟ kuṟiyiṭutali uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu, eṉpatu cūttiram.
 iṉpamum poruḷu... kāṇuṅ kāli eṉpatu -  iṉpamum poruḷum aṟaṉum eṉṟu collappaṭṭu, aṉpoṭu puṇarnta naṭuvaṇ aintiṇiyiṭattu nikaḻum kāmak kūṭṭattiṉi ārāyuṅkālattu  eṉṟavāṟu.
aṉpoṭu puṇarnta aintiṇi eṉṟataṉāṉ, kikkiḷi peruntiṇiyi oḻittu, niṉṟa mulli kuṟiñci pāli marutam neytal eṉak koḷḷappaṭum. avi aṉpoṭu puṇarnta vāṟu eṉṉiyeṉiṉ, kikkiḷi peruntiṇiyip pōlāmi, otta aṉpiṉarākip puṇartalum piritalum iruttalum ūṭalum iraṅkalum nikaḻttupa vākalāṉum, avi nikaḻuṅkāl attiṇikku uṟuppākiya iṭamum kālamum karupporuḷum tuṇiyāki nikaḻu mākalāṉum, ivi aṉpoṭu puṇarntaṉa eṉka. aḵtēl, aintiṇip puṟattavākiya veṭci, vañci, uḻiñi, tumpi, vāki eṉpaṉavum aṉpoṭu puṇarntaṉavām eṉiṉ, avi aṉpuṭiyār palar kūṭi nikaḻttupavi yākaliṉ, aṉpoṭu puṇarntaṉavām.
``aṟattiṟkē yaṉpucār  peṉpa vaṟiyār
maṟattiṟku  maḵtē  tuṇi.’’	(kuṟaḷ. 76)
eṉpataṉāṉum koḷka.
iṉi, ivvintiṇiyum iṉpamum poruḷum aṟamum  āyiṉavāṟu eṉṉiyeṉiṉ puṇartal mutalākiya aintu poruḷum iṉpan tarutaliṉ iṉpamāyiṉa. mulli mutalākiya aintiṇikkum uṟuppākiya nilamum kālamum karupporuḷum ivaṟṟiṉ puṟattākiya veṭci vañci uḻiñi tumpi eṉpaṉavum vākiyuḷ oru kūṟum poruḷākalāṉum, puṇartaṉ mutalākiya uripporuḷāṉ varuvatōr kēṭiṉmiyāṉum, poruḷāyiṉa. ivvoḻukkaṅkaḷ aṟattiṉvaḻi nikaḻtalāṉum, pāliyākiya vākip paṭalattuḷ aṟanili kūṟuta lāṉum, ivi aṟamāyiṉa. aḵtēl, kikkiḷi peruntiṇiyum ivaṟṟiṉ puṟamākiya pāṭāṇpāṭṭum kāñciyum aṟa mutalākiya mūṉṟu maṉṟi aṉpoṭu puṇartal vēṇṭumeṉiṉ, kāñci aṉpoṭu puṇarāmiyum, pāṭāṇ pāṭṭu aṉpoṭu puṇartal orutaliyaṉmi yum avvōttiṉuḷ kaṇṭukoḷka. ēṉiyiraṇṭum aṉpoṭu puṇarāmi mēṟcollappaṭṭaṉa. iṉi avi aṟaṉum poruḷumāy iṉpamākā; aḵtēl, aṟaṉum poruḷum ākāmiyum vēṇṭu meṉiṉ, kulaṉum kuṇaṉuṅ kalviyu muṭiyarākiya antaṇar eṉa vicēṭittavaḻi ēṉiyōrkkum immūṉṟu poruḷum iyital vēṇṭu meṉṉum niyamam iṉmiyiṉ ēṟṟavaḻik koḷḷappaṭum.
iṉi,’ aintiṇi maruṅkiṟ kāmak kūṭṭam’ eṉpatu puṇartal mutalākiya uripporuḷum, annilamum kālamum karupporuḷum, kaḷaviṉum kaṟpiṉum varutaliṉ, avi orōvoṉṟu iruvakip paṭum. avaṟṟuḷ, puṇarcciyākiya iruvakiyiṉum kaḷavākiya kāmakkūṭṭam eṉak koḷka.
iṉṉum ‘aṉpoṭu puṇarnta aintiṇi maruṅkiṟ kāmak kūṭṭam’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ellā nilattiṉum kāmak kūṭṭam nikaḻap peṟum eṉṟu koḷka. avvāṟātal cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷakattuk kāṇka.
maṟiyōr tēetyōriyalpē eṉpatu  maṟiyōriṭat tōtap paṭṭa maṇam eṭṭaṉuḷḷum tuṟiyami nal yāḻiṉi yuṭiyarākiya tuṇimiyōr neṟi  eṉṟavāṟu.
maṟiyōr eṉṟatu antaṇari. tēem eṉṟatu avaratākiya nūli. maṇam eṭṭāvaṉa: piramam, piracāpattiyam, āriṭam, teyvam, kāntiruvam, acuram, irākkatam, picācam eṉpaṉa. piramamāvatu, kaṉṉiyi aṇikalaṉ aṇintu piramacāriyāyiruppāṉoruvaṉukkuttāṉamākak koṭuppatu. piracāpattiyamāvatu, makaṭ kōṭaṟkuriya kōttirattār makaḷ vēṇṭiyavaḻi irumutukuravarum iyintu koṭuppatu. āriṭamāvatu, oṉṟāṉum iraṇṭāṉum āvum āṉēṟum vāṅkik koṭuppatu. teyvamāvatu, vēḷvikku āciriyarāy niṉṟār palaruḷḷum oruvarkku vēḷvittī muṉṉark koṭuppatu. kāntiruva māvatu otta iruvar tāmē kūṭuṅkūṭṭam. acuramāvatu, villēṟṟiṉāṉātal tiripaṉṟi yeytāṉātal kōṭaṟkuriyaṉeṉak kūṟiyavaḻi atu ceytāṟkuk koṭuttal. irākkatamāvatu, talimakaḷ taṉṉiṉum tamariṉum peṟātu valitiṟ koḷvatu. picācamāvatu, kaḷittārmāṭṭun tuyiṉṟārmāṭṭuṅ kūṭutal.
``aṟaṉili  yoppē poruḷkō ṭeyvam
yāḻōr kūṭṭa  marumporuḷ viṉiyē
yirākkatam pēynili yeṉṟik kūṟiya
maṟiyōr maṉṟa leṭṭavi   yavaṟṟuṭ
ṭuṟiyami  nalyāḻt tuṇimiyōr puṇarppiṉataṉ
poruṇmi yeṉmaṉār pulami yōrē.’’
eṉpataṉāluṅ koḷka. tuṟiyami nalyāḻttuṇimi yōrāvār kantiruvar. avar iruvarākit tiritaliṉ ‘tuṇimi yōr’ eṉṟār. 8tuṇiyiyalpāvatu avar oḻukalā ṟōṭottu makkaṇmāṭṭu nikaḻvatu. īṇṭuk kāmak kūṭṭameṉa vōtappaṭṭatu maṇa vikaṟpamākiya eṭṭaṉuḷḷuṅ ‘kantiruva’ meṉṟavāṟu. māli cūṭṭal yātaṉuḷ aṭaṅku meṉiṉ, atuvum otta aṉpiṉarāy nikaḻtaliṟ kantiruvappāṟ paṭum.
aṟaṉum poruḷu miṉpamum eṉṉātu, iṉpamum poruḷum aṟaṉum eṉṟatu eṉṉi (eṉiṉ), palavaki yuyirkaṭkum varum iṉpam iruvakippaṭum. aviyāvaṉa, pōkam nukartalum vīṭu peṟutalum eṉa. avaṟṟuḷ vīṭupēṟu tuṟavaṟattiṉ niṉṟārkkallatu eytal aritāyiṟṟu. pōka nutartal maṉiyaṟattārk keytuvatu. avareytum iṉpamum avviṉpattiṟkuk kāraṇamākiya poruḷum ap poruṭkuk kāraṇamākiya aṟaṉum eṉak kāriya kāraṇam nōkki vittār eṉka.
itaṉāṟ colliyatu īṇṭuk kaḷaveṉṟōtappaṭukiṉṟa oḻukkam aṟampayavāta puṟaneṟiyaṉṟu; vētavitiyākiya tantiram eṉa vikaṟpamākiya neṟi kūṟiyavāṟu.	(1)
90.	oṉṟē vēṟē yeṉṟiru pālvayi
	ṉoṉṟi yuyarnta pāla tāṇiyi
	ṉotta kiḻavaṉuṅ kiḻattiyuṅ kāṇpa
	mikkō ṉāyiṉuṅ kaṭivari yiṉṟē.
eṉ-ṉiṉ, itu kāmakkūṭṭattiṉkaṇ talimakaṉum talimakaḷum etirppaṭun tiṟaṉum ataṟkuk kāraṇamum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
oṉṟē vēṟē eṉṟiru pālvayiṉ eṉpatu - oruvaṉum oruttiyumāka illaṟañ ceytuḻi, avviruvariyum maṟupiṟappiṉum oṉṟuvittalum vēṟākkutalumākiya iruvaki ūḻiṉum  eṉṟavāṟu.
oṉṟiyuyarnta pālatāṇiyiṉ eṉṟatu - iruvaruḷḷamum piṟapputtōṟum oṉṟi nalviṉikkaṇṇē nikaḻnta ūḻiṉatu āṇiyiṉ  eṉṟavāṟu.
uyarntataṉ mēṟcellum maṉanikaḻcci uyarnta pālāyiṟṟu. kāma nikaḻcciyiṉkaṇ otta aṉpiṉarāyk kūṭutal nalviṉiyāṉ allatu vārāteṉpatu karuttu.
otta kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyuṅ kāṇpa eṉpatu: 
oppup pattu vakippaṭum. (avi),
``piṟappē kuṭimi yāṇmi yāṇṭō
ṭuruvu niṟutta kāma vāyi
ṉiṟiyē yaruḷē yuṇarvoṭu tiruveṉa
muṟiyuṟak kiḷanta voppiṉa vakiyē.’’	(meyp. 25)
eṉṉum meyppāṭṭiyaṟ cūttirattuḷ (kūṟiya) pattumām. avaṟṟuḷ piṟappāvatu, antaṇar, aracar, vaṇikar, vēḷāḷar, āyar, vēṭṭuvar, kuṟavar, nuḷiyar eṉṟāṟpōla varuṅ kulam. kuṭimiyāvatu ak kulattiṉuḷḷār ellāruñ ciṟappāka ovvāmiyiṉ acciṟappākiya oḻukkam paṟṟiya (kuṭivaravu) kuṭivaravik ‘kuṭimi’ eṉṟār.
``piṟappokkum ellā vuyirkkum ciṟappovvā
ceytoḻil vēṟṟumi yāṉ.’’	(kuṟaḷ. 972)
eṉap piṟarum kulattiṉkaṇṇē ciṟappeṉpatu oṉṟuṇṭeṉṟu kūṟiṉārākaliṉ. āṇmiyāvatu, āṇmittaṉmi. aḵtāvatu, āḷviṉiyuṭimiyum vali peyarāmiyumām.
``moḻiyā tataṉi muṭṭiṉṟu muṭittal’’	(marapiyal. 110)
eṉpataṉāṉ talimakaḷmāṭṭup peṇmiyum koḷḷappaṭum. atu peṇṭirkku iyalpākiya nāṇam mutalāyiṉavum peṇṇīrmiyum. āṇṭeṉpatu, oruvariṉoruvar mutiyaraṉṟi otta paruvattarātal: atu kuḻavipparuvaṅ kaḻintu patiṉāṟu pirāyattāṉum paṉṉiraṇṭu pirāyattāḷumātal. uruvu eṉpatu vaṉappu. niṟutta kāmavāyil eṉpatu niliniṟuttap paṭṭa puṇarccikku vāyil; aḵtāvatu, oruvar māṭṭu oruvarkku nikaḻum aṉpu. niṟi eṉpatu aṭakkam. aruḷ eṉpatu piṟar varuttattiṟkup puriyum karuṇi. uṇarveṉpatu aṟivu. tiru eṉpatu celvam. ippattu vakiyum otta kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyum etirppaṭuvar eṉak koḷka.
mikkōyiṉṟē eṉpatu - ikkuṇaṅkaḷāl talimakaṉ mikkāṉāyiṉuṅ kaṭiyappaṭātu  eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, ivaṟṟuḷ yātāṉum oṉṟāyiṉum talimakaḷ mikkāḷāyiṉ aintiṇiyiṟ kaṭiyappaṭum eṉṟavāṟām.
pālatāṇiyiṟ... kāṇpa eṉpatu - oruvari yoruvar kaṇṭuḻi yellām puṇarcci vēṭki tōṟṟāmiyiṟ pālatāṇiyāṉ oruvariyoruvar puṇartaṟ kuṟippoṭu kāṇpa  eṉṟavāṟu. mikkō ṉāyiṉum eṉṟa ummi iṟantatu taḻīiya ecca vummi. eṟṟukku, etirmaṟiyākki ‘iḻintō ṉāyiṉum kaṭiyappaṭātu’ eṉṟāṟ kuṟṟa meṉṉiyeṉiṉ, centamiḻ nilattu vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi varukiṉṟa perumiyātaliṉ, iḻintāṉoṭu uyarntāṭkuḷatākiya kūṭṭamiṉmi peruvaḻakkātaliṉ atu poruḷākak koḷḷām eṉpatu. īṇṭuk kiḻava ṉum kiḻattiyum eṉa oruvaṉum oruttiyum pōlak kūṟiṉārāyiṉum,
``orupāṟ kiḷavi yēṉippāṟ kaṇṇum
varuvaki tāṉē vaḻakkeṉa moḻipa.’’	(poruḷiyal. 26)
eṉpataṉāṉum, innūl ulakavaḻakkē nōkkutalāṉum, avar pala vakippaṭuvar. aḵtāmāṟu antaṇar aracar vaṇikar vēḷāḷar eṉṉum nālvarōṭum anulōmar aṟuvariyum kūṭṭap patiṉmarāvar. ivari nālvaki nilattōṭu uṟaḻa nāṟpatiṉmarāvar. 9ivariyum avvannilattiṟkuriya āyar vēṭṭuvar kuṟavar paratavar eṉṉun toṭakkattāroṭu kūṭṭap palarāvar. avariyum uyirp paṉmiyāṉ nōkka varampilarāvar.	(2)
91.	ciṟantuḻi yiyañ ciṟanta teṉpa
	viḻintuḻi yiḻivē cuṭṭa lāṉa.
eṉ-ṉiṉ, aiyam nikaḻum iṭam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
oruvaṉ oruttiyik kaṇṇuṟṟuḻi avviru vakiyarum uyarvuṭiyarāyiṉ avviṭattu aiyam ciṟantatu eṉṟu colluvar; avar iḻipuṭiyarāyiṉ, avviṭattu avaḷ (iḻi)piṉiyē cuṭṭi yuṇartalāṉ  eṉṟavāṟu. ciṟappu eṉpatu mikuti. aiyamikutalāvatu makkaḷuḷḷāḷ allaḷ teyvamō eṉa mēlāyiṉārōṭē aiyuṟutal. ciṟantuḻi eṉpataṟkut talimakaḷ tāṉ ciṟantuḻiyum koḷḷappaṭum; avaḷik kaṇṭa iṭam aiyappaṭutaṟkuc ciṟantuḻiyum koḷḷappaṭum. uruva mikuti yuṭiyaḷātaliṉ āyattāriṭik kāṇiṉum teyvam eṉṟu aiyuṟutal. itaṉāṟ colliyatu ulakattuttalimakaṉum talimakaḷumāka nammāl vēṇṭappaṭṭār antaṇar mutalākiya nāṉku varuṇattiṉum āyar vēṭṭuvar kuṟavar paratavar eṉṉum toṭakkattiṉum akkulattārākiya kuṟunila maṉṉar māṭṭum uḷarāvaraṉṟē? avarel lāriṉum celvattāṉum kulattāṉum vaṭivāṉum uyarnta talimaka ṉum talimakaḷu (māyiṉōr) māṭṭē aiyam nikaḻvatu. allātār māṭṭu av viḻimarapiṉiyē cuṭṭiyuṇarā niṟkumātalāṉ  eṉṟavāṟu.
‘kārārap peyta’ eṉṉum mullik kaliyuḷ 	(kalit. 109)
``maṇṇit tamartan tōrpuṟan tiiya
kaṇṇi  yeṭukkallāk kōṭēn  takalalkul
puṇṇilār puṇṇāka nōkku muḻumeyyuṅ
kaṇṇaḷō vāyar makaḷ.’’
eṉa aiyamiṉṟic cuṭṭiyuṇarntavāṟu kāṇka.
iṉi uyarpuḷvaḻi aiyam nikaḻumāṟu:
``uyarmoḻik kiḷavi yuṟaḻuṅ kiḷavi
yiyak kiḷavi yāṭūuviṟ  kurittē’’	(poruḷiyal. 43)
eṉṟārākaliṉ, aiyappaṭuvāṉ talimakaṉ eṉṟu koḷka. talimakaḷ aiyappaṭātateṉṉiyeṉiṉ, avaḷ aiyappaṭuṅ kāl teyvamō veṉṟu aiyuṟal vēṇṭum. avvāṟu aiyuṟṟāl accam varum. aḵtu ētuvākak kāma nikaḻcci yuṇṭākātu.	(3)
92.	vaṇṭē yiḻiyē vaḷḷi pūvē
	kaṇṇē yalamara limippē yaccameṉ
	ṟaṉṉavi piṟavu  māṅka  ṇikaḻa
	niṉṟavi kaḷiyuṅ karuvi yeṉpa.
eṉ-ṉiṉ, aiyappaṭṭāṉ tuṇitaṟkuk karuvi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
eṇṇappaṭṭa vaṇṭu mutalākiya eṭṭum 10piṟavumāki avviṭattu nikaḻāniṉṟavi aiyam kaḷiyum karuvi  eṉṟavāṟu.
aiyameṉpatu atikārattāṉ vantatu. ‘nikaḻā niṉṟavi’ eṉpatu kuṟukiniṉṟatu. vaṇṭāvatu mayiriṉ aṇinta pūvic cūḻum vaṇṭu. atu payiṉṟataṉ mēlallatu cellāmiyiṉ atuvum makkaḷuḷḷā ḷeṉṟaṟitaṟkuk karuviyāyiṟṟu. iḻiyeṉpatu aṇikalaṉ. atu ceyyappaṭṭateṉat tōṟṟutalāṉum, teyvappūṇ ceyyā aṇiyātalāṉum atuvum aṟitaṟkuk karuviyāyiṟṟu. vaḷḷi eṉpatu muliyiṉum tōḷiṉum eḻutiya koṭi. atuvum ulakiṉ uḷḷatākit tōṉṟutaliṉ (atuvum) karuviyāyiṟṟu. alamaral eṉpatu taṭumāṟutal. teyvamāyiṉ niṉṟavaḻi niṟkum. avvā ṟaṉṟi, niṉṟuḻi niṟkiṉṟilaḷ eṉṟu cuḻaṟciyum aṟitaṟkuk karuviyāyiṟṟu. imippeṉpatu kaṇṇimittal. teyvattiṟkuk kaṇ imiyāmiyiṉ atuvum aṟitaṟkuk karuviyāyiṟṟu. acca meṉpatu āṇmakkaḷik kaṇṭu añcutal. atu teyvattiṟku iṉmiyāṉ atuvum aṟitaṟkuk karuviyāyiṟṟu. aṉṉavi piṟavum eṉṟataṉāṉ kālnilantōytal, viyarttal, niḻalā(ṭu)tal koḷka. ivi karuviyākattuṇiyappaṭum  eṉṟavāṟu.
kāṭci mutalākiya ittuṇiyum kikkiḷik kuṟippām. itaṟkuc ceyyuḷ vantavaḻik kāṇka. iṉik kuṟippaṟital kūṟukiṉ ṟārākaliṉ, akkuṟippu nikaḻumvaḻi iviyellām akamām? eṉṉi; iruvar māṭṭumotta nikaḻcci yātalāṉ. ivi talimakaḷ māṭṭup pulappaṭa nikaḻātu. āṇṭuk kuṟippiṉāṟ ciṟitu nikaḻumeṉṟu koḷka.
avi varumāṟu:
``uṇṭārka  ṇalla  taṭunaṟāk kāmampōṟ
kaṇṭār makiḻceyta  liṉṟu.’’	(kuṟaḷ. 1090)
eṉavum varum. piṟavum aṉṉa.	(4)
93.	nāṭṭa  miraṇṭu maṟivuṭam paṭuttaṟkuk
	kūṭṭi yurikkuṅ kuṟippuri yākum.
eṉ-ṉiṉ mēl talimakaḷi ittaṉmiyaḷ eṉat tuṇinta talimakaṉ kuṟippaṟiyātu cāraluṟiṉ peruntiṇip pāṟpaṭu mākalāṉum ik kantiruvaneṟikku otta uḷḷattārātal vēṇṭumātalāṉum, āṇṭu oruvarōṭoruvar collāṭutal marapaṉmiyāṉum, avaruḷḷak karuttaṟital vēṇṭutaliṉ, ataṟkuk karuviyāya uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
nāṭṭamiraṇṭum eṉpatu - talimakaṉ kaṇṇum talimakaḷ kaṇṇum  eṉṟavāṟu. aṟivuṭampaṭuttaṟku eṉpatu - oruvar vēṭki pōla iruvarkkum vēṭki uḷatākuṅ kollō eṉak kavarttu niṉṟa iruvaratu aṟiviṉiyum oruppaṭuttuṟku  eṉṟavāṟu. kūṭṭiyurikkuṅ kuṟippuriyākum eṉpatu - tamatu vēṭki yoṭu kūṭṭi oruvaroruvarkku urikkuṅ kāmak kuṟippuriyām  eṉṟavāṟu.
itaṉ poḻippu: iruvarkkuṅ kavarttu niṉṟa aṟivi orup paṭuttaṟporuṭṭu vēṭkiyoṭu kūṭṭik kūṟuṅ kāmak kuṟippuc col iruvaratu nāṭṭamākum  eṉṟavāṟu.
ākum eṉpataṉi nāṭṭam eṉpataṉoṭu kūṭṭiyurikka. itaṟkuc ceyyuḷ:
``pāṉalan 11taṇkaḻip pāṭaṟintu taṉṉiyar
nūṉala nuṇvaliyāṟ toṇṭeṭutta -  kāṉaṟ
paṭupulāl kāppāḷ paṭineṭuṅka ṇōkkaṅ
kaṭipollā veṉṉiyē kāppu.’’	(tiṇimāli. 32)
kaṇṇiṉāṉ aṟipa  eṉṟavāṟu.	(5)
94.	kuṟippē kuṟittatu koḷḷu māyi
	ṉāṅkavi nikaḻu meṉmaṉār pulavar.
eṉ-ṉiṉ, mēlataṟkōr puṟaṉaṭi yuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
oruvar kuṟippu oruvar kuṟittataṉik koḷḷu māyiṉ, avviṭattuk kaṇṇiṉāṉ varuṅ kuṟippuri nikaḻum  eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, kuṟippik koḷḷātavaḻi akkuṟippuri nikaḻātu eṉṟavāṟām. itaṉāṟ colliyatu kaṇṭa kālattē vēṭki muntuṟṟa vaḻiyē ikkaṇṇiṉāṉ varuṅ kuṟippu nikaḻvatu; allātavaḻi nikaḻātu  eṉṟavāṟu. iṉik kuṟippu nikaḻumāṟum ataṉ vēṟupāṭum meyppāṭ ṭiyaluḷ kūṟupa. īṇṭum cila utāraṇam: kāṭṭutum.
``nōkkiṉā ṇōkki yiṟiñciṉā ḷaḵtavaḷ
yāppiṉu ḷaṭṭiya nīr.’’	(kuṟaḷ. 1093)
eṉavum,
``aciyiyaṟ kuṇṭāṇṭō rēeriyā ṉōkkap
paciyiṉaḷ piya nakum.’’	(kuṟaḷ. 1098)
eṉavum varum. piṟavum aṉṉa. talimakaṉkuṟipput talimakaḷ aṟinta vaḻiyum kūṟṟu nikaḻātu, peṇmiyāṉ.	(6)
95.	perumiyu muraṉu māṭūu mēṉa.
eṉ-ṉiṉ, itu talimakaṟku uriyatōr ilakkaṇa muṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
perumiyāvatu - paḻiyum pāvamum añcutal. uraṉ eṉpatu  aṟivu.
ivi yiraṇṭum āṇmakaṉukku iyalpu  eṉṟavāṟu.
itaṉāṉē mēṟcollappaṭṭa talimakaḷatu vēṭkik kuṟippuk kaṇṭa talimakaṉ, anniliyē puṇarcciyi niṉiyātu, varintu eytumeṉpatu peṟutum.
``ceṉṟa viṭattāṟ celaviṭā tītorīi
naṉṟiṉpā luyppa taṟivu.’’	(kuṟaḷ. 422)
eṉpavākaliṉ. talimakaṉ ivvāṟu kūṟiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ:
``vēyeṉat tiraṇṭatōḷ veṟikamaḻ vaṇarimpāṉ
māṉveṉṟa maṭanōkkiṉ mayiliyalāṟ ṟaḷarpolki
yāycilam pariyārppa vaviroḷi yiḻiyimippak
koṭiyeṉa miṉṉeṉa vaṇaṅkeṉa yātoṉṟun
terikallā viṭiyiṉkaṭ kaṇkavar poruṅkōṭa
vaḷamicā luyarciṟappi ṉuntitol viyaṉaka
riḷamiyā ṉeṟipantō ṭikattantāy kēḷiṉi;
pūntaṇṭārp pularcāntiṟ ṟeṉṉava ṉuyarkūṭaṟ
ṟēmpāya vaviḻnīlat talarveṉṟa vamaruṇkaṇ
ṇēntukōṭ ṭeḻilyāṉi 12yoṉṉātārk kavaṉvēliṟ
cēntunī yiṉiyiyā lottatō ciṉmoḻi;
poḻipeyal vaṇmiyā ṉacōkantaṇ kāviṉuṭ
kaḻikavi ṉiḷamāviṉ ṟaḷiraṉṉā yataṉṟalip
13paṇiyami pāymāṉṟē ravaṉceṟṟār niṟampāynta
kaṇiyiṉu nōyceytal kaṭappaṉṟō kaṉaṅkuḻāy;
vakiyami taṇṭārāṉ kōṭuyar poruppiṉmēṟ
ṟakiyiṇa riḷavēṅki malaraṉṉa cuṇaṅkiṉāy
matavali  kamaḻkaṭāat tavaṉyāṉi maruppiṉuṅ
katavavāṟ ṟakkatō kāḻkoṇṭa  viḷamuli;
eṉavāṅku,
iṉiyaṉa kūṟa viṟiñcupu nilanōkki
niṉiyuku neṭitoṉṟu niṉippāḷpōṉ maṟṟāṅkē
tuṇiyami tōḻiyark kamartta kaṇṇaḷ
maṉiyāṅkup peyarntāḷe ṉaṟivakap paṭuttē.’’	(kalit. 57)
``uṟukaḻi maruṅki ṉōtamoṭu malarnta
ciṟukaru neytaṟ kaṇpōṉ  māmalarp
peruntaṇ māttaḻi 14yirunta valku
liya varumpiya cuṇaṅkiṉ viyeyiṟṟu
miyī rōti vāṇutaṟ kuṟumakaḷ
viḷiyā ṭāyamoṭu veṇmaṇa  lukutta
puṉṉi nuṇṭātu poṉṉi ṉoṇṭu
maṉipuṟan taruti yāyi   ṉeṉiyatūu
mimmaṉik kiḻami 15yemmoṭu puṇariṟ
ṟītu  muṇṭō māta rāyeṉak
kaṭumpari  naṉmāṉ koṭiñci neṭuntēr
kival pākaṉ piyeṉa viyakka
yāntaṟ kuṟukiṉa māka vēnteḻi
larivē  yuṇkaṇ paṉivara loṭukkic
ciṟiya viṟiñciṉa ṭaliyē
periya vevvam  16yāmiva ṇuṟavē.’’	(akam. 230)
ivi uḷḷap puṇarcciyāṉ varital vēṇṭip pāṅkaṟku 17urittaṉa.	(7)
96.	accamum nāṇu maṭaṉu muntuṟuta
	ṉiccamum peṇpāṟ kuriya veṉpa.
eṉ-ṉiṉ, itu talimakaṭku uriyatōr ilakkaṇam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
accamum nāṇum pēti(mi)yum immūṉṟum nāṭōṟu muntuṟutal peṇṭirkku iyalpu  eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, vēṭkiyuṟṟuḻiyum accattāṉātal nāṇāṉātal maṭattāṉātal puṇarccikku iciyātu niṉṟu varinteytal vēṇṭu meṉpatu pōntatu. ivvāṟu iruvarum uḷḷap puṇarcciyāl niṉṟu varinteyti meyyuṟum. itaṟkuc ceyyuḷ:
``tīmpāl kaṟanta kalamāṟṟik kaṉṟellān
tāmpiṟ piṇittu maṉiniṟīi yāytanta
pūṅkari nīlam puṭitāḻa meyyaciip pāṅkaru
mulliyun tāya pāṭṭaṅkāṟ ṟōḻinam
pulliṉat tāyar makaḷirō ṭellā
mōrṅku viḷiyāṭa vavvaḻi vanta
kuruntampūṅ kaṇṇip potuvaṉmaṟ ṟeṉṉi
muṟṟiḻi yēer maṭanallāy nīyāṭuñ
ciṟṟil puṉikō ciṟiteṉṟā ṉellānī
peṟṟēmyā meṉṟu piṟarceyta villiruppāy
kaṟṟa tilimaṉṟa kāṇeṉṟēṉ muṟṟiḻāy
tātucūḻ kūnta 18ṟakipeṟat tiiya
kōti puṉikō niṉakkeṉṟā ṉellānī
ētilār tantapūk koḷvāy naṉimikap
pētiyi maṉṟa periteṉṟēṉ mātarā
yiya pitirnta cuṇaṅkaṇi meṉmulimēṟ
ṟoyyi  leḻutukō maṟṟeṉṟāṉ yāmpiṟar
ceypuṟa nōkki yiruttumō nīperitu
miyali mātō viṭuveṉṟēṉ ṟiyalāy
colliya vāṟellā māṟumā 19ṟiyāṉpeyarppa
vallāntāṉ pōlap peyarntā ṉavaṉinī
yāyar makaḷi riyalpurit tentiyum
yāyu maṟiya vurittīyiṉ yāṉuṟṟa
nōyuṅ kaḷikuvi maṉ.’’	(kalit. 111)
itu talimakaḷ uḷḷappuṇarcciyiṉ urimipūṇṭirunta vāṟum varinteytak kūṟaluṟṟavāṟum kāṇka.	(8)
97.	vēṭki yorutali yuḷḷutaṉ melita
	lākkañ ceppa ṉāṇuvari yiṟatta
	ṉōkkuva vellā maviyē pōṟaṉ
	maṟattaṉ mayakkañ cākkā ṭeṉṟic
	ciṟappuṭi marapiṉavi kaḷaveṉa moḻipa.
eṉ-ṉiṉ, meyyuṟupuṇarcci nikaḻuṅkālam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
mēl,’’perumiyu muraṉu māṭūu mēṉa’’ (95) eṉavum,
``20accamum nāṇu maṭaṉum muntuṟutal
niccamum peṇpāṟ kuriya veṉpa.’’	
eṉavum ōtiyavataṉāṉ uḷḷañ ceṉṟavaḻiyum meyyuṟu puṇarcci varinteyti nikaḻttupa veṉṟārām. avvaḻic cākkāṭelliyākiya meyppāṭuvariṉ meyyuṟṟup puṇarappeṟu meṉpatu uṇarttiṟṟu.
vēṭki mutalākac cākkāṭu īṟāka ōtappaṭṭa kāmac ciṟappuṭiyavāṟṟāṟ kaḷavu āmeṉṟu colluvar  eṉṟavāṟu.
āṉum āmum eñci niṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟi avattiyeṉpa. aḵtēl, avi pattuḷavaṉṟē? īṇṭurittaṉa oṉpatā leṉiṉ, kāṭci vikaṟpamuṅ kūṟiṉār, aḵtu uṭpaṭap pattām. kāṭci vikaṟpamākiya aiyamun tuṇivum mutalatu; vēṭki iraṇṭāvatu, eṉṟu koḷka.
vēṭkiyāvatu - peṟalvēṇṭu meṉṉum uḷḷa nikaḻcci.
orutaliyuḷḷuta lāvatu - iṭiviṭātu niṉittal.
``uḷḷik kāṇpeṉ pōlveṉ muḷḷeyiṟ
ṟamiḻta mūṟuñ cevvāyk kamaḻaki
lāra nāṟu maṟalpōṟ kūntaṟ
pēramar maḻikkaṭ koṭicci
mūraṉ muṟuvaloṭu matiiya nōkkē.’’	(kuṟun. 286)
melitalāvatu - uṇṇāmiyāṉ varuvatu.
ākkañceppa lāvatu - uṟaṅkāmiyum uṟuva vōtalum mutalāyiṉa kūṟutal.
``oṇṭoṭi yarivi koṇṭaṉa ṇeñcē
vaṇṭimir paṉittuṟit toṇṭi yāṅka
ṇuravukkaṭa  lolittiri pōla
viravi ṉāṉun tuyilaṟi yēṉē’’	(aiṅkuṟu. 172)
eṉa varum.
nāṇuvariyiṟattalāvatu - nāṇ nīṅkutal.
``kāmam viṭuvoṉṟō nāṇviṭu naṉṉeñcē
yāṉō poṟēṉiv viraṇṭu.’’	(kuṟaḷ. 1247)
nōkkuva vellām aviyē pōṟa lāvatu - taṉṉāṟ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa ellān tāṉ kaṇṭa uṟuppup pōlutal.
``ōṅkeḻiṟ kompar naṭuvi teṉappulluṅ
kāntaṭ kivarum karuviḷam pūkkoḷḷu
māntaḷir kiyiṟ  ṟaṭavaru māmayil
pūmpoḻi ṉōkkip pukuvaṉa piṉcellun
tōḷeṉac ceṉṟu tuḷaṅkoḷi vēytoṭu
nīḷkatup piḵteṉa nīraṟa  luṭpukum.’’ eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa.
maṟattal - pittātal. mayakkamāvatu - mōkittal. cākkāṭu - cātal. ivaṟṟuḷ cātal pattām avattiyātalāl, oḻinta eṭṭuṅ kaḷavu nikaḻtaṟkuk kāraṇamām eṉṟu koḷka. itu talimakaṭkum tali makaṟkum okkum. ivaṟṟiṟkuc ceyyuḷ vantavaḻik kāṇka.	(9)
98.	muṉṉili yākkal colvaḻip paṭutta
	ṉaṉṉaya muritta ṉakinaṉi yuṟāa
	vannili yaṟitaṉ melivuviḷak kuṟutta
	ṟaṉṉili yuritta ṟeḷivakap paṭuttaleṉ
	ṟiṉṉavi nikaḻu meṉmaṉār pulavar.
eṉ-ṉiṉ, iḵtu iyaṟkip puṇarccikkuriyatōr tiṟaṉ uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
taṉiyiṉāl talimakaḷi yetirppaṭṭa talimakaṉ taṉṉuṭiya perumiyum aṟivum nīkki vēṭki mītūrap puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉāṉāyiṉum, talimakaḷmāṭṭu niṟkum accamum nāṇum maṭaṉum nīkkutalum 21vēṇṭumaṉṟē. avi nīṅkutaṟporuṭṭu iviyellām nikaḻumeṉpatu. ulakat tuḷḷā rilakkaṇa mellām urikkiṉṟārākaliṉ, ivvāciriyar urikkiṉṟavāṟṟāṉ nikaḻtal perumpāṉmi yākavum,
``colliya nukarcci vallē peṟṟuḻi’’	(tol. kaḷaviyal. 11)
eṉa ōtutaliṉ, iviyellām nikaḻtaliṉṟic ciṟupāṉmi vēṭki mikutiyāṟ puṇarcci kaṭitiṉ muṭiyavum peṟumeṉavuṅ koḷka.
muṉṉiliyākkal eṉpatu - kāmakkuṟippuṇmi aṟinta talimakaṉ vēṭkiyāṟ cāra niṉittavaḻit talimakaḷum vēṭkik kuṟippuṭiyā ḷāyiṉum kulattiṉ vaḻivanta iyaṟkiyāṉ nāṇamum accamum 22mītūra ak kuṟippillātārip pōl niṉṟavaḻi avaḷi muṉṉiliyākap paṭuttuc cila kūṟutal.
 ``oḷḷiḻi makaḷiro ṭōriyu māṭāy
vaḷḷitaḻ neytaṟ ṟoṭaliyum puriyāy
viripūṅ kāṉa  loruciṟi niṉṟō
yāri yōniṟ ṟoḻutaṉam viṉavutuṅ
kaṇṭār taṇṭā nalattit teṇṭirip
peruṅkaṭaṟ parappi ṉamarntuṟi yaṇaṅkō
viruṅkaḻi maruṅki  ṉilipeṟ ṟaṉiyō
colliṉi maṭanti yeṉṟaṉe ṉataṉetir
muḷḷeyiṟṟu muṟuvalun tiṟantaṉa
pallita ḻuṇkaṇum parantavāṟ paṉiyē.’’	(naṟ. 155)
colvaḻip paṭuttalāvatu - tāṉ collukiṉṟa colliṉvaḻi avaḷ niṟkumāṟu paṭuttuk kūṟutal.
``colliṟ colletir koḷḷā yāḻaniṉ
ṟirumuka miṟiñci nāṇuti katumeṉak
kāmaṅ 23kimmikiṟ ṟāṅkuta leḷitō
koṭuṅkē ḻirumpuṟa naṭuṅkak kuttip
puliviḷi yāṭiya pukarmuka  vēḻattiṉ
ṟalimarup pēyppak kaṭimaṇi civantaniṉ
kaṇṇē katava valla naṇṇā
rāṇṭali matila rākavu muracukoṇ
ṭōmparaṇ kaṭanta vaṭupōrc ceḻiyaṉ
perumpeyark  kūṭa  laṉṉaniṉ
karumpuṭit tōḷu muṭiyavā  laṇaṅkē.’’	(naṟ. 39)
naṉṉaya murittalāvatu - talimakaḷiṉatu nalattiṉi yurittal.
``cēraṉ maṭavaṉṉañ cēraṉṉaṭi yovvāy
cēraṉ maṭavaṉṉañ cēraṉṉaṭi yovvā
yūrtiri vēli yuḻakkit tirivāḷpiṉ
cēraṉ maṭavaṉṉañ cēraṉṉaṭi yovvāy.’’	(cilap. kāṉal. 23)
nakinaṉi yuṟāa anniliyaṟitalāvatu - talimakaṉ taṉ naṉṉaya murittalik kēṭṭa talivikku iyalpāka akattu uḷavākum makiḻvāṟ puṟantōṉṟum muṟuvaṟ kuṟippu mikkut tōṉṟā anniliyiṉit talivaṉ aṟital.
``māṇiḻi pēti nāṟiruṅ kūnta
lāṇamu  millā  ṇīruṟi cūruṭic
cilampiṟ kaṇaṅkāy muyaṉṟa ceṟiyiya
ṉotuma  ṉōkkik kāṇmō neñcē
vaṟitāṉ muṟuvaṟ keḻumiya
nuṭaṅkumeṉ paṇivēy ciṟukuṭi yōḷē.’’
melivu viḷakkuṟuttalāvatu - talivaṉ akattuṟum nōyāṟ puṟattu nikaḻum taḷarviṉik kuṟippāṉ eṭuttuk kūṟalum. utāraṇam: vantuḻik kāṇka.
kuṟippāvaṉa puṟattuṟuppā yavark 24kiṉṟiyamiyātaṉa.
taṉṉili yurittalāvatu - ap puṟanikaḻcciyiṉ poli viḻavik kaṇṭa talimakaṇmāṭṭuttalivaṉ taṉ uḷḷa vēṭki mītūrviṉi nilipaṭak kūṟutal.
``colliṟ colletir koḷḷā yāḻaniṉ’’	
eṉṉum naṟṟiṇippāṭṭuḷ,
``kāmaṅ kimmikiṟ ṟāṅkuta  leḷitō
.........	...........   ...........  ...........
......  ........... kaṭimaṇi civantaniṉ
kaṇṇē katava valla.’’	(naṟ. 39)
eṉattaṉṉili yurittuvāṟu kāṇka.
teḷivu akappaṭutta lāvatu - talivaṉ muṉṉili yākkal mutaliyaṉa kūṟip piṉṉar iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉi viḻintu niṉṟāṉāka, ap puṇarcciyiṉik kūṟuvār, muṉṉam otta paṇpuṭimi uḷḷattu iruvarmāṭṭum vēṇṭutaliṉ, talimakaḷ paṇpiṉittalivaṉ aṟintu atteḷivit taṉṉakappaṭuttut tērtal.
``yāyum yāyum yārā kiyarō’’
eṉṉum kuṟuntokip pāṭṭuḷ,
``aṉpuṭi neñcan tāṅkalan taṉavē’’	(kuṟun. 40)
eṉa iyaṟkip puṇarcci muṉṉarttalivaṉ taliviyar uḷḷam otta paṇpiṉik kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka.
itukāṟum iyaṟkip puṇarccikkuriya tiṟaṉkūṟi, mēl iyaṟkip puṇarcci nikaḻumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟār. aḵtēl, taṉṉili yurittalit teḷivakappaṭuttaluṭaṉ iṇittu meyyuṟu puṇarcci yākkiya piṉṉart tōṉṟun tuṟiyākap paṭuttālō eṉiṉ, atu cāṉṟōr vaḻakkiṉṟātalāṉum, meyyuṟu puṇarcci muṉ,
``meytoṭṭup payiṟal’’	(kaḷaviyal. 11)
mutaliyaṉa yāṇṭum nikaḻntē talivikku meyyuṟu puṇarcci nikaḻumātalāṉum, aḵtaṉṟeṉka.	           (10)
99.	meytoṭṭup payiṟal poypā rāṭṭa
	liṭampeṟṟut taḻāa liṭiyūṟu kiḷattal
	nīṭuniṉin tiraṅkal kūṭuta luṟutal
	colliya nukarcci vallē peṟṟuḻit
	tīrāt tēṟṟa muḷappaṭat tokiip
	pērāc ciṟappi ṉirunāṉku kiḷaviyum
	peṟṟavaḻi makiḻcciyum pirintavaḻik kalaṅkalu
	niṟpavi niṉii nikaḻpavi yurippiṉuṅ
	kuṟṟaṅ kāṭṭiya vāyil peṭpiṉum
	peṭṭa vāyilpeṟ ṟiravuvali yuṟuppiṉu
	mūrum pēruṅ keṭutiyum piṟavu
	nīriṟ kuṟippi ṉirampak kūṟit
	tōḻiyik kuṟiyuṟum pakutiyun tōḻi
	kuṟiyavaṭ cārtti meyyuṟak kūṟalun
	taṇṭā tirappiṉu maṟṟiya vaḻiyuñ
	collavaṭ cārttaliṟ pulliya vakiyiṉu
	maṟintō  ḷayarppi ṉavvaḻi maruṅkiṟ
	kēṭum pīṭuṅ kūṟalun tōḻi
	nīkkali ṉākiya nilimiyu nōkki
	maṭaṉmā kūṟu miṭaṉumā ruṇṭē.
eṉ-ṉiṉ, kaḷaviṟ kūṭṭam nāṉkiṉiṭattum talivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻumāṟum, kātal mikku āṟṟāmi kiyikappiṉ talivaṉām iyalum kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
meytoṭṭup payiṟalāvatu - perumiyum uraṉumuṭiya talimakaṉ teḷivakappaṭuttiyatu kāraṇamākak kātal veḷḷam puraṇṭōṭat taliviyiṉ meyyit tīṇṭip payiṟal.
``tīṇṭalu miyivatu kollō māṇṭa
villuṭi yiḷiyar kalliṭu peṭutta
naṉantalik kāṉat tiṉantalip pirinta
puṉkaṇ  maṭamā ṉērpaṭat  taṉṉiyar
cilimāṇ  kaṭuvicik kaliniṟat  taḻuttik
kurutiyoṭu paṟitta  ceṅkōl vāḷi
māṟukoṇ ṭaṉṉa  vuṇka
ṇāṟiruṅ kūntaṟ koṭicci tōḷē.’’	(kuṟun. 272)
poy pārāṭṭalāvatu - taliviyiṉ aimpāl mutaliya kaṭi kuḻaṉṟu citiviṉṟēṉum aḵtuṟṟatāka maruṅkuceṉṟu toṭṭāṉ ōr kāraṇam poyyākap paṭittu urittup pārāṭṭal.
``kaṭalpuk kuyirkoṉṟu vāḻvarniṉ ṉiya
ruṭalpuk kuyirkoṉṟu vāḻvimaṉ  ṉīyu
miṭalpuk kaṭaṅkāta vemmuliyō pāra
miṭarpuk kiṭuku  miṭiyiḻaval kaṇṭāy.’’	(cilap. kāṉal. 17)
iṭampeṟṟuttaḻāa lāvatu - poypārāṭṭal kāraṇamāt talivimāṭṭu 25aṇimiyiṭam peṟṟuttaḻuvak kūṟal. utāraṇam:
``kolyāṉi veṇmaruppuṅ kolval puliyataḷu
nalyāṇar  niṉṉiyar kūṭṭuṇṭu - celvārtā
mōrampi ṉāleytu pōkkuvaryāṉ pōkāmi
yīrampi ṉāleytā  yiṉṟu.’’	(tiṇimāli. 22)
iṭiyūṟu kiḷatta lāvatu - nāṇ maṭaṉ nilikkaḷaṉāk koṇṭa talivi taṉ aṟivunalaṉ iḻantu oṉṟum aṟiyātu uyirttaṉaḷ. aḵtu okkumō eṉiṉ okkum. putitāyp pukkār, ūṟṟuṇarvu eṉṟum payilāta tam melliyal meyyiṟpaṭa aṟiviḻappiṉum 26uḷ nekku uyirkkum eṉka. atupaṟṟip puliyaṉ toṭu tīmpāl pōl kātal kūrak kompāṉum koṭiyāṉum cārntāḷit talivaṉ, ``ippoḻutu ivvūṟṟiṉpiṟku iṭiyūṟāy niṉmaṉattakattu nikaḻntavi yāveṉa viṉavutalum.
nīṭuniṉintiraṅkalāvatu - iruvar iyalum oruṅku iṇintum talivi perunāṇāl pālvaḻi uṟukaveṉa eṇṇi māṟṟamuṅ kuṟiyuṅ kāṭṭātu kaṇ putippāḷit talivaṉ puṟamōcci niṟkavum āṇṭum kalakkalām poḻutu kūṭāmikku niṉintu iraṅkal.
kūṭutaluṟutalāvatu - iṅṅaṉamāyk kāṭci nikaḻviṉ piṉṉarp puṇarcci eytalum.
ituvari iyaṟkip puṇarcciyām kāraṇaṅ kūṟik kūṭutaluṟutalāṉ meyyuṟu puṇarcci kūṟiṉār. ivaṟṟiṟkuc ceyyuḷ:
``vīḻu miruvark kiṉitē vaḷiyiṭi
pōḻap paṭāa muyakku.’’	(kuṟaḷ. 1108)
itu kūṭutaluṟutal. piṟa vantuḻik kāṇka.
